%    4 - EL RANGO DE ACTIVIDADES DEBE ESTAR % 1 Y 99                    0000002600026000  
%    5 - SE INTENTA INICIAR LA ACTIVIDAD ESTANDO PROCESANDOLA           0000002700027000

%   12 - LOS NUMEROS DE TIPO DE PROCESO DEBEN ESTAR % 1 Y 10            0000003600036000  
%   13 - LA ACTIVIDAD DEBE ESTAR EN PROCESO                             0000003700037000  
%   14 - EL TIPO DE DESPLIEGUE DE INFORMACION DEBE SER:                 0000003800038000  
%        1 - INFORMACION TIPO LIBRERIA                                  0000003900039000  
%        2 - INFORMACION TIPO ACTIVIDADES                               0000004000040000  
%   15 - EN NUMERO DE ACTIVIDAD DEBE SER < A 100 O LA ACTIVIDAD DESDE   0000004100041000  
%        LA QUE SE ESTA LLAMANDO DEBE ESTAR EN PROCESO.                 0000004200042000  
%   16 - NO PUEDE HABER ACTIVIDADES TIPO PROCESO ANIDADAS               0000004300043000

%   18 - EL INCIO DE GRUPO DEBE SER IGUAL A NUMERO DE TIPO DE           0000004500045000  
%        PROCESO.                                                       0000004600046000  
%   19 - PARA DAR INICIO O FIN DE ACTIVIDAD EL PROGRAMA DEBE ESTAR      0000004700047000

%********************************************************************** 0000040100390000  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(I_INPUT);                         0000040200391000  
 DOUBLE I_INPUT;                                                        0000040300392000  
%********************************************************************** 0000040400393000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000040500394000  
   DEFINE ID_SEGUNDOS = (I_INPUT DIV 1000000)#;                         0000040600395000  
   INTEGER I_DIAS,                                                      0000040700396000  
           I_HORAS,                                                     0000040800397000  
           I_MINUTOS,                                                   0000040900398000  
           I_SEGUNDOS,                                                  0000041000399000  
           I_CENT_SEGUNDO;                                              0000041100400000  
                                                                        0000041200401000  
%  I_DIAS   := ID_SEGUNDOS DIV (3600 * 24);                             0000041300402000  
   I_DIAS   := 0;                                                       0000041400403000  
   I_HORAS  := (ID_SEGUNDOS DIV 3600) - (I_DIAS * 24);                  0000041500404000  
   I_MINUTOS:= (ID_SEGUNDOS DIV 60)   - (I_DIAS * 24 * 60)              0000041600405000  
                                      - (I_HORAS * 60);                 0000041700406000  
   I_SEGUNDOS:= ID_SEGUNDOS - (I_DIAS * 24 * 3600)                      0000041800407000  
                            - (I_HORAS * 3600    )                      0000041900408000  
                            - (I_MINUTOS * 60    );                     0000042000409000  
   I_CENT_SEGUNDO:= (I_INPUT MOD 1000000) DIV 10000;                    0000042100410000  
                                                                        0000042200411000  
   I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS:= I_DIAS          * 100000000 +                   0000042300412000  
                        I_HORAS         * 1000000   + % FTO HHHHMMSSCC  0000042400413000  
                        I_MINUTOS       * 10000     + % YA QUE DIAS     0000042500414000  
                        I_SEGUNDOS      * 100       + % QUEDO EN CERO   0000042600415000  
                        I_CENT_SEGUNDO;                                 0000042700416000  
 END;                                                                   0000042800417000  
                                                                        0000042900418000  
                                                                        0000043000419000  
%********************************************************************** 0000043100420000  
 DOUBLE  PROCEDURE D_PROC_ET(I_FECH_INI,                                0000043200421000  
                             I_HORA_INI,                                0000043300422000  
                             I_FECH_FIN,                                0000043400423000  
                             I_HORA_FIN);                               0000043500424000  
 INTEGER  I_FECH_INI,                                                   0000043600425000  
          I_HORA_INI,                                                   0000043700426000  
          I_FECH_FIN,                                                   0000043800427000  
          I_HORA_FIN;                                                   0000043900428000  
%********************************************************************** 0000044000429000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000044100430000  
   DOUBLE D_ET;                                                         0000044200431000  
                                                                        0000044300432000  
   IF I_FECH_INI = I_FECH_FIN THEN                     % Mismo dia      0000044400433000  
      D_ET:= I_HORA_FIN - I_HORA_INI                                    0000044500434000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000044600435000  
   IF (I_FECH_INI DIV 100) = (I_FECH_FIN DIV 100) THEN % Mismo mes      0000044700436000  
      D_ET:= ((I_FECH_FIN MOD 100) -                                    0000044800437000  
              (I_FECH_INI MOD 100)) * 24 * 3600 * 1000000               0000044900438000  
                                                       % Dias en mmseg  0000045000439000  
            + (I_HORA_FIN - I_HORA_INI)                % Dif en la horas0000045100440000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000045200441000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000045300442000  
                                                                        0000045400443000  
%  En cualquier otro caso se debe llamar a THECALENDAR para obtener     0000045500444000  
%  la diferencia en dõas. No se llama siempre para no alentar los       0000045600445000  
%  procesos                                                             0000045700446000  
     INTEGER      RESULT,I_FUNCION_TH;                                  0000045800447000  
     EBCDIC ARRAY EA_FECH1,EA_FECH2[0:7];                               0000045900448000  
                                                                        0000046000449000  
     REPLACE EA_FECH1 BY I_FECH_FIN FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000046100450000  
     REPLACE EA_FECH2 BY I_FECH_INI FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000046200451000  
     I_FUNCION_TH:= 2;                                                  0000046300452000  
     THECALENDAR(I_FUNCION_TH,EA_FECH1,EA_FECH2,13);                    0000046400453000  
     IF I_FUNCION_TH = 0 THEN                                           0000046500454000  
        D_ET := INTEGER(EA_FECH1,8) * 24 * 3600 * 1000000               0000046600455000  
                                                       % Dias en mmseg  0000046700456000  
                + I_HORA_FIN - I_HORA_INI              % Dif en la hora 0000046800457000  
     ELSE                                                               0000046900458000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000047000459000  
%      DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                    0000047100460000  
%      REPLACE EA_MSG BY "ERROR AL LLAMAR A THECALENDAR";               0000047200461000  
%      DF_DISPLAY(EA_MSG);                                              0000047300462000  
       REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "ERROR AL LLAMAR A THECALENDAR";             0000047400462100  
       LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                                  0000047500462200  
     END;                                                               0000047600463000  
   END;                                                                 0000047700464000  
                                                                        0000047800465000  
   D_PROC_ET:= D_ET;                                                    0000047900466000  
 END;                                                                   0000048000467000  
                                                                        0000048100468000  
%********************************************************************** 0000048200469000  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE I_PROC_PJEACT(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI);              0000048300470000  
 INTEGER I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI;                                       0000048400471000  
%********************************************************************** 0000048500472000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000048600473000  
   IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000048700474000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACT_NOINI                         THEN                  0000048800475000  
      I_PROC_PJEACT:= 0                                                 0000048900476000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000049000477000  
   IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)   =                      0000049100478000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                              THEN              0000049200479000  
      IF DF_DA_REGS_APROC  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) > 0 THEN           0000049300480000  
         I_PROC_PJEACT:= DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) /  0000049400481000  
                         DF_DA_REGS_APROC  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) *  0000049500482000  
                         100                                            0000049600483000  
      ELSE                                                              0000049700484000  
         I_PROC_PJEACT:= 0                                              0000049800485000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000049900486000  
   IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000050000487000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACT_TERM                          THEN                  0000050100488000  
      I_PROC_PJEACT:= 100;                                              0000050200489000  
 END;                                                                   0000050300490000

          ,I_RESULT                                                     0000051100498000

  PROCEDURE BEGINPROG;                                                  0000052000507000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000052100508000  
   EXCEPTION PROCEDURE LIBERA;                                          0000052200509000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000052300510000  
     IF LOCKSTATUS (LK_BEGINPROG).[47:24] = PROCESSID THEN              0000052400511000  
        UNLOCK(LK_BEGINPROG);                                           0000052500512000  
   END;                                                                 0000052600513000  
                                                                        0000052700514000  
                                                                        0000052800515000  
   PROCEDURE VALIDA;                                                    0000052900516000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000053000517000  
     IF SIZE(EA_ENTRADA) < 48 THEN                                      0000053100518000  
        I_RESULT:= 17;                                                  0000053200519000  
                                                                        0000053300520000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000053400521000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000053500522000  
       REPLACE E_SCR_BP BY " " FOR SIZE(E_SCR_BP);                      0000053600523000  
       REPLACE E_SCR_BP BY MYSELF.USERCODE;                             0000053700524000  
       I_USER_CORRIDA := INTEGER(E_SCR_BP[1],3);                        0000053800525000  
       REPLACE E_SCR_BP BY " " FOR SIZE(E_SCR_BP);                      0000053900526000  
       REPLACE E_SCR_BP BY MYSELF.NAME;                                 0000054000527000  
       I_NUM_PGM      := INTEGER(E_SCR_BP[19],3);                       0000054100528000  
       I_NUM_COPIA    := IDF_PI_COPIA_E;                                0000054200529000  
       IF E_SCR_BP[18] = "P" THEN                                       0000054300530000  
          I_TIPO_PGM:= DF_DP_TPGM_PROG                                  0000054400531000  
       ELSE                                                             0000054500532000  
       IF E_SCR_BP[18] = "L" THEN                                       0000054600533000  
          I_TIPO_PGM:= DF_DP_TPGM_LIB                                   0000054700534000  
       ELSE                                                             0000054800535000  
          I_TIPO_PGM:= DF_DP_TPGM_INVA;                                 0000054900536000  
                                                                        0000055000537000  
       I_NUM_TIPO_PROC:= IDF_PI_NUM_TIPO_PROC_E;                        0000055100538000  
     END;                                                               0000055200539000  
                                                                        0000055300540000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000055400541000  
        IF I_NUM_PGM < 1   OR                                           0000055500542000  
           I_NUM_PGM > 999 THEN                                         0000055600543000  
           I_RESULT:= 11;                                               0000055700544000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000055800545000  
        IF I_NUM_COPIA < 1   OR                                         0000055900546000  
           I_NUM_COPIA > 15 THEN                                        00000560          
           I_RESULT:= 1;                                                0000056100548000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000056200549000  
        IF I_TIPO_PGM = DF_DP_TPGM_INVA THEN                            0000056300550000  
           I_RESULT:= 2;                                                0000056400551000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000056500552000  
        IF I_NUM_TIPO_PROC < 1  OR                                      0000056600553000  
           I_NUM_TIPO_PROC > 10 THEN                                    0000056700554000  
           I_RESULT:= 12;                                               0000056800555000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000056900556000  
        IF NOT (IDF_PI_DSP_INFO_E = DF_PI_DSP_TIPO_LIB OR               0000057000557000  
                IDF_PI_DSP_INFO_E = DF_PI_DSP_TIPO_ACT   ) THEN         0000057100558000  
           I_RESULT:= 14;                                               0000057200559000  
     IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000057300560000  
        IF IDF_PI_INICIO_GPO_E > 0 THEN                                 0000057400561000  
           IF NOT (I_NUM_TIPO_PROC = IDF_PI_INICIO_GPO_E ) THEN         0000057500562000  
              I_RESULT:= 18;                                            0000057600563000  
                                                                        0000057700564000  
   END;                                                                 0000057800565000  
                                                                        0000057900566000  
   PROCEDURE LLENAARRAYS;                                               0000058000567000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000058100568000  
                                                                        0000058200569000  
     IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                 0000058300570000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000058400571000  
       DISPLAY ("L035: USER CORRIDA: " !!                               0000058500572000  
                STRING(I_USER_CORRIDA,*));                              0000058600573000

                STRING(I_NUM_PGM,*));                                   0000058800575000

       DISPLAY ("L035: TIPO PGM: " !!                                   0000059100578000  
                STRING(I_TIPO_PGM,*));                                  0000059200579000  
     END;                                                               0000059300580000  
                                                                        0000059400581000  
   I_INDICE_PGM:= LA_PROGS[I_USER_CORRIDA,                              0000059500582000  
                           I_NUM_PGM,                                   0000059600583000  
                           I_NUM_COPIA,                                 0000059700584000  
                           I_TIPO_PGM];                                 0000059800585000  
                                                                        0000059900586000  
   IF I_INDICE_PGM = 0 THEN                           % Programa nuevo  0000060000587000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000060100588000  
     I_INDICE_PGM:= SIZE(LA_DATOS_PROGS[*,*]);        % No progs dados  0000060200589000  
                                                                        0000060300590000  
     IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                 0000060400591000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000060500592000  
       DISPLAY ("L035:PROG INI: PROGRAMA NO DADO DE ALTA");             0000060600593000

                STRING(I_INDICE_PGM,6));                                0000060800595000  
     END;                                                               0000060900596000  
                                                                        0000061000597000  
     LA_PROGS[I_USER_CORRIDA,                         % de alta + 1     0000061100598000  
              I_NUM_PGM,                                                0000061200599000  
              I_NUM_COPIA,                                              0000061300600000  
              I_TIPO_PGM]     := I_INDICE_PGM;                          0000061400601000  
     RESIZE(LA_DATOS_PROGS[*,*],(I_INDICE_PGM + 1), RETAIN);            0000061500602000  
     RESIZE(LA_DATOS_ACT  [*,*,*],(I_INDICE_PGM + 1), RETAIN);          0000061600603000  
                                                                        0000061700604000  
     LA_USERS[I_USER_CORRIDA]:= 1;                    % User nuevo en   0000061800605000  
                                                      % uso             0000061900606000  
                                                                        0000062000607000  
     % Se llenan datos de arreglo de Agrupadores X User                 0000062100608000  
%    DF_AXU_NUM_TPO_PROC(I_USER_CORRIDA,I_NUM_TIPO_PROC):=              0000062200609000  
%                  I_NUM_TIPO_PROC;                                     0000062300610000  
%    REPLACE DF_AXU_NOM_TPO_PROC(I_USER_CORRIDA,I_NUM_TIPO_PROC) BY     0000062400611000  
%            DF_PI_NOM_TIPO_PROC_E FOR 40,                              0000062500612000

 PROCEDURE GRABABASE (I_IND_PGM);                                       0000080300787000  
 INTEGER I_IND_PGM;                                                     0000080400788000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000080500789000  
   EXCEPTION PROCEDURE LIBERA;                                          0000080600790000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000080700791000  
     IF LOCKSTATUS (LK_ENDPROG).[47:24] = PROCESSID THEN                0000080800792000  
        UNLOCK(LK_ENDPROG);                                             0000080900793000  
   END;                                                                 0000081000794000  
                                                                        0000081100795000  
   REAL    R_DMERROR;                                                   0000081200796000  
   BOOLEAN B_ERROR;                                                     0000081300797000  
   INTEGER I_IND_ACT;                                                   0000081400798000  
                                                                        0000081500799000  
   BEGINTRANSACTION NOAUDIT S038B91REINICIO:R_DMERROR;                  0000081600800000  
                                                                        0000081700801000  
   LOCK B02SXIDPGM                                                      0000081800802000  
         AT B02-USER-CORRIDA = DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA(I_IND_PGM) AND        0000081900803000  
            B02-NUM-PGM      = DF_DP_NUM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM) AND        0000082000804000  
            B02-NUM-COPIA    = DF_DP_NUM_COPIA   (I_IND_PGM) AND        0000082100805000  
            B02-TIPO-PGM     = DF_DP_TIPO_PGM    (I_IND_PGM) :R_DMERROR;0000082200806000  
   IF NOT BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                       0000082300807000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000082400808000  
   IF R_DMERROR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                                 0000082500809000  
     CREATE S038B02PROGRAMAS:R_DMERROR                                  0000082600810000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000082700811000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000082800812000  
%    DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                      0000082900813000  
%    REPLACE EA_MSG BY "ERROR LOCK B02SXIDPGM";                         0000083000814000  
%    DF_DISPLAY(EA_MSG);                                                0000083100815000  
     REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "ERROR LOCK B02SXIDPGM";                       0000083200815100  
     LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                                    0000083300815200  
     B_ERROR:= TRUE;                                                    0000083400816000  
   END;                                                                 0000083500817000  
   IF NOT(B_ERROR) THEN                                                 0000083600818000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000083700819000  
     PUT S038B02PROGRAMAS                                               0000083800820000  
         (B02-USER-CORRIDA := DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM)            0000083900821000  
         ,B02-NUM-PGM      := DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM)            0000084000822000  
         ,B02-NUM-COPIA    := DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM)            0000084100823000

         ,B02-NOM-PGM      := DF_DP_NOM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM)            0000084300825000  
         ,B02-ESTATUS-PGM  := DF_DP_PGM_TERMINADO(I_IND_PGM)            0000084400826000  
         ,B02-TIPO-DSPINFO := DF_DP_TIPO_DSPINFO (I_IND_PGM)            0000084500827000  
         ,B02-FECH-INICIO  := DF_DP_FECH_INI     (I_IND_PGM)            0000084600828000  
         ,B02-HORA-INICIO  := DF_DP_HORA_INI     (I_IND_PGM)            0000084700829000  
         ,B02-FECH-TERMINA := DF_DP_FECH_TER     (I_IND_PGM)            0000084800830000  
         ,B02-HORA-TERMINA := DF_DP_HORA_TER     (I_IND_PGM)            0000084900831000  
         ,B02-NUM-MIX      := DF_DP_NUM_MIX      (I_IND_PGM)            0000085000832000  
         ,B02-NUM-TPO-PROC := DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)            0000085100833000  
         ,B02-NOM-TPO-PROC := DF_DP_NOM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)            0000085200834000  
         ,B02-ET-ANTERIOR  := DF_DP_ET_ANTERIOR  (I_IND_PGM)            0000085300835000  
         ,B02-ET-ACTUAL    := DF_DP_ET_ACTUAL    (I_IND_PGM)            0000085400836000  
         ,B02-NUM-CORRIDA  := DF_DP_NUM_CORRIDA  (I_IND_PGM)            0000085500837000  
         ,B02-INICIO-GPO   := DF_DP_INIGPO       (I_IND_PGM)            0000085600838000  
         ,B02-INDICE-PGM   := LA_PROGS[DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),  0000085700838100  
                                       DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM),  0000085800838200  
                                       DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM),  0000085900838300  
                                       DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM)]);0000086000838400  
                                                                        0000086100839000  
     STORE S038B02PROGRAMAS:R_DMERROR;                                  0000086200840000  
     IF BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                         0000086300841000  
        B_ERROR:= TRUE;                                                 0000086400842000  
   END;                                                                 0000086500843000  
   IF NOT(B_ERROR) THEN                                                 0000086600844000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000086700845000

          DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          THEN              0000087300851000

           DF_DP_FECH_TER  (I_IND_PGM);                                 0000087700855000  
        DF_DA_HORA_TER(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):=                           0000087800856000  
           DF_DP_HORA_TER  (I_IND_PGM);                                 0000087900857000  
        DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) :=                       0000088000858000  
           DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) +                     0000088100859000  
           D_PROC_ET(DF_DA_FECH_INI(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT),               0000088200860000  
                     DF_DA_HORA_INI(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT),               0000088300861000  
                     DF_DA_FECH_TER(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT),               0000088400862000  
                     DF_DA_HORA_TER(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT));              0000088500863000  
      END;                                                              0000088600864000  
                                                                        0000088700865000  
      IF (DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) =                        0000088800866000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          OR               0000088900867000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) =                        0000089000868000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACT_TERM                          OR               0000089100869000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) =                        0000089200870000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACT_NOINI                           ) THEN         0000089300871000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000089400872000  
       LOCK B03SXIDACT AT                                               0000089500873000  
            B03-USER-CORRIDA  = DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA(I_IND_PGM) AND       0000089600874000  
            B03-NUM-PGM       = DF_DP_NUM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000089700875000  
            B03-NUM-COPIA     = DF_DP_NUM_COPIA   (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000089800876000  
            B03-TIPO-PGM      = DF_DP_TIPO_PGM    (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000089900877000  
            B03-NUM-ACTIVIDAD = DF_DA_NUM_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,           0000090000878000  
                                                   I_IND_ACT)           0000090100879000  
            :R_DMERROR;                                                 0000090200880000  
       IF NOT BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                   0000090300881000  
       ELSE                                                             0000090400882000  
       IF R_DMERROR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                             0000090500883000  
         CREATE S038B03ACTIVIDAD:R_DMERROR                              0000090600884000  
       ELSE                                                             0000090700885000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000090800886000  
%        DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                  0000090900887000  
%        REPLACE EA_MSG BY "ERROR LOCK B03SXIDACT";                     0000091000888000  
%        DF_DISPLAY(EA_MSG);                                            0000091100889000  
         REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "ERROR LOCK B03SXIDACT";                   0000091200889100  
         LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                                0000091300889200  
         B_ERROR:= TRUE;                                                0000091400890000  
       END;                                                             0000091500891000  
       IF NOT(B_ERROR) THEN                                             0000091600892000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000091700893000  
        PUT S038B03ACTIVIDAD                                            0000091800894000  
         (B03-USER-CORRIDA  := DF_DA_USER_CORRIDA(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000091900895000  
         ,B03-NUM-PGM       := DF_DA_NUM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092000896000  
         ,B03-NUM-COPIA     := DF_DA_NUM_COPIA   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092100897000  
         ,B03-TIPO-PGM      := DF_DA_TIPO_PGM    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092200898000  
         ,B03-NUM-ACTIVIDAD := DF_DA_NUM_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092300899000  
         ,B03-NOM-ACTIVIDAD := DF_DA_NOM_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092400900000  
         ,B03-ESTATUS-ACT   := DF_DA_STA_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092500901000  
         ,B03-REGS-APROC    := DF_DA_REGS_APROC  (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092600902000  
         ,B03-NUM-LLAMADOS  := DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092700903000  
         ,B03-TPO-ACTIVIDAD := DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT)  0000092800904000

 PROCEDURE ACTUREG;                                                     0000124801209000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000124901210000  
                                                                        0000125001211000  
   DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E,IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E) :=            0000125101212000  
    DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E,IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E) + 1;         0000125201213000  
                                                                        0000125301214000  
   REPLACE DF_DA_TXT_ADIC(IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E,IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E) BY        0000125401215000  
           DF_AR_TXT_ADIC_E FOR 40,                                     0000125501216000  
           " "              FOR  2;                                     0000125601217000  
 END;                                                                   0000125701218000  
                                                                        0000125801219000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000125901220000  
% OUTER BLOCK S038L035_05_ACTU_REG_C                                    0000126001221000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000126101222000  
%  REPLACE EA_SALIDA BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_SALIDA);                     0000126201223000  
                                                                        0000126301224000  
   IF SIZE(EA_ENTRADA) < 52 THEN                                        0000126401225000  
      I_RESULT:= 17;                                                    0000126501226000  
                                                                        0000126601227000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000126701228000  
      IF IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E > (SIZE(LA_DATOS_PROGS[*,*]) - 1 ) OR         0000126801229000  
         IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E = 0                                THEN       0000126901230000  
         I_RESULT:= 3;                                                  0000127001231000  
                                                                        0000127101232000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000127201233000  
      IF IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E < 1  OR                                      0000127301234000  
         IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E > 99 THEN                                    0000127401235000  
         I_RESULT:= 4;                                                  0000127501236000  
                                                                        0000127601237000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000127701238000  
   IF NOT (DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E,IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E) =        0000127801239000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                                   ) THEN  0000127901240000  
      I_RESULT:= 13;                                                    0000128001241000  
                                                                        0000128101242000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000128201243000  
      IF DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(IDF_AR_ID_PROG_E,IDF_AR_NUM_ACTI_E) =          0000128301244000  
         DF_DA_TPA_ACT_LIB THEN                                         0000128401245000  
         I_RESULT:= 8;                                                  0000128501246000  
                                                                        0000128601247000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000128701248000  
      ACTUREG;                                                          0000128801249000  
                                                                        0000128901250000  
   S038L035_05_ACTU_REG_C:= I_RESULT;                                   0000129001251000  
                                                                        0000129101252000  
 END OF S038L035_05_ACTU_REG_C;                                         0000129201253000  
                                                                        0000129301254000

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000199821001000  
%                                                                      %0000199921002000  
%             PROPERTY DEFINES IN ALPHABETICAL ORDER                   %0000200021003000  
%                                                                      %0000200121004000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000200221005000  
%                                                                      %0000200321006000  
%            INSTRUCTIONS FOR ADDING NEW PROPERTY DEFINES              %0000200421007000  
%                                                                      %0000200521008000  
%      IN  THE  INTEREST OF KEEPING THESE PROPERTIES READABLE,  PLEASE %0000200621009000  
% ADD NEW DEFINES MAKING SURE TO:                                      %0000200721010000  
%                                                                      %0000200821011000  
%         1) KEEP LIST IN ALPHABTICAL ORDER                            %0000200921012000  
%         2) START NEW DEFINES IN SAME COLUMN AS EXISTING ENTRIES      %0000201021013000  
%         3) KEEP "=" AND "%" SIGNS ALIGNED WITH EXISTING ENTRIES      %0000201121014000  
%         4) ALWAYS INCLUDE A USAGE LIST FOR THE NEW DEFINE ON THE     %0000201221015000  
%            SAME LINE AS THE DEFINE                                   %0000201321016000  
%         5) PUT IN A COMMENT DESCRIBING THE DEFINE ON THE LINES       %0000201421017000  
%            FOLLOWING THE USAGE LIST. ALWAYS START COMMENTS IN        %0000201521018000  
%            COLUMN 43.                                                %0000201621018500  
%                                                                      %0000201721019000  
%      THE ADDITION OF THE USAGE LIST TO THESE PROPERTY DEFINES ALLOWS %0000201821020000  
% THE PRECISE DEFINITION OF WHERE EVERY PROPERTY APPLIES, AS WELL  AS, %0000201921021000  
% FACILITATING THE  CREATION  OF  THE PROPERTIES DIRECTORY STARTING AT %0000202021022000  
% LINE  16000000.   THIS  DIRECTORY  HAS  FOR  EVERY  DESCRIPTION FILE %0000202121023000  
% STRUCTURE A LIST  OF  APPLICABLE  PROPERTIES  IN STORAGE ORDER.  THE %0000202221024000  
% ACCEPTABLE MNEMONICS THAT MAY APPEAR IN THE USAGE LIST ARE DESCRIBED %0000202321025000  
% BELOW.                                                               %0000202421026000  
%                                                                      %0000202521027000  
% MNEMONIC                 PROPERTY OF                                 %0000202621028000  
% --------                 -------- --                                 %0000202721029000  
%  DB                      DATABASE                                    %0000202821030000  
%  D                       DATASET STRUCTURE                           %0000202921031000  
%  S                       SET STRUCTURE                               %0000203021032000  
%  R                       REMAP STRUCTURE                             %0000203121033000  
%  DITEM                   DATASET IN AN ITEM LIST                     %0000203221034000  
%  SITEM                   SET IN AN ITEM LIST                         %0000203321035000  
%  RITEM                   REMAP IN AN ITEM LIST                       %0000203421036000  
%  LDB                     LOGICAL DATABASE                            %0000203521037000  
%  ALPH                    ALPHA ITEM                                  %0000203621038000  
%  GRP                     GROUP ITEM                                  %0000203721039000  
%  BOLN                    BOOLEAN ITEM                                %0000203821040000  
%  FBIT                    BOOLEAN IN A FIELD                          %0000203921041000  
%  FLD                     FIELD ITEM                                  %0000204021042000  
%  DECI                    INTEGER NUMBER ITEM ( NUMBER(I) )           %0000204121043000  
%  DECF                    FIXED POINT NUMBER ITEM ( NUMBER(I,J) )     %0000204221044000  
%  BINI                    INTEGER REAL ITEM ( REAL(I) )               %0000204321045000  
%  BINF                    FIXED POINT REAL ITEM ( REAL(I,J) )         %0000204421046000  
%  BFLT                    FLOATING POINT REAL ITEM ( REAL )           %0000204521047000  
%  POPN                    POPULATION ITEM                             %0000204621048000  
%  FILLR                   FILLER ITEM                                 %0000204721049000  
%  CNT                     COUNT ITEM                                  %0000204821050000  
%  TYP                     RECORD TYPE ITEM                            %0000204921051000  
%  LNK                     LINK ITEM                                   %0000205021052000  
%  AUDIT                   AUDIT TRAIL                                 %0000205121053000  
%  CF                      CONTROL FILE                                %0000205221054000  
%  TXT                     SET TEXTNODE                                 0000205321054500  
%  ALL                     ALL TYPES LISTED ABOVE                      %0000205421055000  
%  ALLITEMS                ALL ITEM TYPES EXCEPT DITEM,RITEM,SITEM     %0000205521056000  
%  ALLSTRS                 ALL STRUCTURE TYPES (D,R,S)                 %0000205621057000  
%  OVERRIDE                CAUTION - THIS OVERRIDES OVERLAP CHECKING   %0000205721058000  
%                          -------   FOR THIS PROPERTY                 %0000205821059000  
%                                                                      %0000205921060000  
%      THE  MNEMONICS  ALL,ALLITEMS,ALLSTRS  ALLOW THE ASSIGNING OF  A %0000206021061000  
% PROPERTY TO A WHOLE  CLASS  OF  TYPES  AT ONCE.  A MINUS SIGN MAY BE %0000206121062000  
% USED  TO  INDICATE  TYPES  WHICH  DO NOT FALL INTO THAT CLASS.   FOR %0000206221063000  
% EXAMPLE:                                                             %0000206321064000  
%                                                                      %0000206421065000  
%  ,PROPERTYNAME               = NN].[XX:YY #  % (ALLITEMS-POPN)       %0000206521066000  
%                                                                      %0000206621067000  
%      THIS USAGE LIST  WOULD DENOTE THAT "PROPERTYNAME" IS A PROPERTY %0000206721068000  
% OF ALL  ITEMS  EXCEPT POPULATION ITEMS.  ALL MNEMONICS FOLLOWING THE %0000206821069000  
% MINUS  SIGN (EXCEPT OVERRIDE) SHOULD BE TYPES FOR WHICH THE PROPERTY %0000206921070000  
% DOES NOT APPLY.                                                      %0000207021071000  
%      ALL PROPERTIES  FOR  A GIVEN TYPE ARE CHECKED FOR OVERLAPS WHEN %0000207121072000  
% THE PROPERTY DIRECTORY IS CREATED.  A CARD  GIVING A WARNING MESSAGE %0000207221073000  
% IS EMITTED AFTER EVERY OVERLAPPING  PROPERTY.   THIS  FEATURE CAN BE %0000207321074000  
% OVERRIDEN  BY USING THE MNEMONIC "OVERRIDE" IN THE USAGE LIST  OF  A %0000207421075000  
% PROPERTY  WHERE AN  OVERLAP IS ALLOWABLE. (EXAMPLE:   "IDENTIFIERSZ" %0000207521076000  
% OVERLAPS WITH "WORDONE")                                             %0000207621077000  
%                                                                      %0000207721078000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000207821079000  
                                                                        0000207921080000

           DATOS1[0:29]                                                 0000357801319000

    DEFINE                                                              0000359401335000  
                                                                        0000359501336000  
     CFACRNAMETEXT           = 1#   % ACCESSROUTINES CODEFILE TITLE     0000359601337000  
    ,CFTASKEQUATIONTEXT      = 2#   % TASK EQUATION STRING              0000359701338000  
    ,CFCOPYAUDITPRIWFLTEXT   = 4#   % PRIMARY COPYAUDIT WFL DECK TITLE  0000359801339000  
    ,CFCOPYAUDITSECWFLTEXT   = 5#   % SECONDARY COPYAUDIT WFL DECK TITLE0000359901340000

    ,CFREORGNAMETEXT         =14#   % REORGANIZATION CODEFILE TITLE     0000360801349000  
    ,CFRMSUPPORTNAMETEXT     =15#   % RMSUPPORT CODEFILE TITLE          0000360901350000  
    ,CFDDASERVERNAMETEXT     =16#   % RDASERVER CODEFILE TITLE          0000361001351000

    ,PROCIORESULTADO         =                                          0000361401355000  
     IOERR := BUFF.IOERRORTYPE;                                         0000361501356000  
     IF IOERR = 7 OR IOERR = 0 THEN                                     0000361601357000  
     ELSE                                                               0000361701358000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000361801359000  
       RESULTADO := 5;                                                  0000361901360000  
       POBLACION := 0;                                                  0000362001361000  
       REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "IO ERROR ", IOERR FOR 3 DIGITS,             0000362101362000  
                            ": ",LINENUMBER FOR 8 DIGITS;               0000362201363000  
       LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                                  0000362301364000  
%      DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                    0000362401364100  
%      REPLACE EA_MSG BY "IO ERROR ", IOERR FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000362501364200  
%                         ": ",LINENUMBER FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000362601364300  
%      DF_DISPLAY(EA_MSG);                                              0000362701364400  
       GO ETIQDEERROR;                                                  0000362801365000  
     END;#;                                                             0000362901366000

    PROCEDURE READCF;                                                   0000363201369000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000363301370000  
      READ (CF,300,ARRONE);                                             0000363401371000  
      TEXTSZ := ARRONE[41].[47:16];                                     0000363501372000  
      TEXTST := ARRONE[41].[31:16];                                     0000363601373000  
      STRST := ARRONE[20].[31:16];                                      0000363701374000  
      STRSZ := ARRONE[20].[47:16];                                      0000363801375000  
      CLOSE (CF,REWIND);                                                0000363901376000  
      FOR I := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL TEXTST DO                                 0000364001377000  
        READ (CF,300,ARRONE);                                           0000364101378000  
      READ(CF,300,ARRTWO);                                              0000364201379000  
      X := 0;                                                           0000364301380000  
      M := MASKSEARCH(0 & (CFCONTROLGUARDFILETEXT) CFNUMF,              0000364401381000  
                      0 & REAL(NOT FALSE) CFNUMF,ARRTWO[TEXTSZ-1]);     0000364501382000  
      IF M NEQ -1 THEN                                                  0000364601383000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000364701384000  
        I := ARRONE[X];                                                 0000364801385000  
        X := * + (I DIV 6) + 1;                                         0000364901386000  
        IF (I MOD 6) NEQ 0 THEN X := * + 1;                             0000365001387000  
      END;                                                              0000365101388000  
      M := MASKSEARCH(0 & (CFACRNAMETEXT) CFNUMF,                       0000365201389000  
                      0 & REAL(NOT FALSE) CFNUMF,ARRTWO[TEXTSZ-1]);     0000365301390000  
      IF M NEQ -1 THEN                                                  0000365401391000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000365501392000  
        I := ARRONE[X];                                                 0000365601393000  
       X := * + (I DIV 6) + 1;                                          0000365701394000  
        IF (I MOD 6) NEQ 0 THEN X := * + 1;                             0000365801395000  
      END;                                                              0000365901396000  
      M := MASKSEARCH(0 & (CFDDASERVERNAMETEXT) CFNUMF,                 0000366001397000  
                      0 & REAL(NOT FALSE) CFNUMF,ARRTWO[TEXTSZ-1]);     0000366101398000  
      IF M NEQ -1 THEN                                                  0000366201399000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000366301400000  
        I := ARRONE[X];                                                 0000366401401000  
        X := * + (I DIV 6) + 1;                                         0000366501402000  
        IF (I MOD 6) NEQ 0 THEN X := * + 1;                             0000366601403000  
      END;                                                              0000366701404000  
      M := MASKSEARCH(0 & (CFDDASERVER2NAMETEXT) CFNUMF,                0000366801405000  
                    0 & REAL(NOT FALSE) CFNUMF,ARRTWO[TEXTSZ-1]);       0000366901406000  
      IF M NEQ -1 THEN                                                  0000367001407000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000367101408000  
        I := ARRONE[X];                                                 0000367201409000  
        X := * + (I DIV 6) + 1;                                         0000367301410000  
        IF (I MOD 6) NEQ 0 THEN X := * + 1;                             0000367401411000  
      END;                                                              0000367501412000  
      M := MASKSEARCH(0 & (CFDMSUPPORTNAMETEXT) CFNUMF,                 0000367601413000  
                      0 & REAL(NOT FALSE) CFNUMF,ARRTWO[TEXTSZ-1]);     0000367701414000  
      IF M NEQ -1 THEN                                                  0000367801415000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000367901416000  
        I := ARRONE[X];                                                 0000368001417000  
        PA := POINTER(ARRONE[X+1]);                                     0000368101418000  
      END;                                                              0000368201419000  
                                                                        0000368301420000  
      REPLACE DMSUPPORT.TITLE BY PA FOR I;                              0000368401421000  
                                                                        0000368501422000  
%     DISPLAY(PA);                                                      0000368601423000  
                                                                        0000368701424000  
      CLOSE(CF,LOCK);                                                   0000368801425000  
      FOR K := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL STRST  DO                                 0000368901426000  
        READ(CF,300,ARRTWO);                                            0000369001427000  
      CONT := STRSZ;                                                    0000369101427050  
      CONT1 := 0;                                                       0000369201427100  
      IF STRSZ > 299 THEN                                               0000369301427150  
      BEGIN                                                             0000369401427200  
        CONT1 := * + 1;                                                 0000369501427250  
        RESIZE(ARRTWO,STRSZ+1,RETAIN);                                  0000369601427300  
        WHILE CONT > 299 DO                                             0000369701427350  
        BEGIN                                                           0000369801427400  
          READ(CF,300,ARRTWO[300*CONT1]);                               0000369901427450  
          CONT := STRSZ - 300;                                          0000370001427500  
        END;                                                            0000370101427550  
      END;                                                              0000370201427600  
      CURRBLK := ARRTWO[0].[31:16];                                     0000370301428000  
      CLOSE(CF,LOCK);                                                   0000370401429000  
      FOR K := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL CURRBLK DO                                0000370501430000  
        READ(CF,300,ARRONE);                                            0000370601431000  
      FOR I := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL STRSZ-1 DO                                0000370701432000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000370801433000  
        LABEL MOREINFO;                                                 0000370901434000  
        IF (I+1) MOD 300 = 0 THEN                                       0000371001434100  
           STRSZ := * + 1                                               0000371101434200  
        ELSE                                                            0000371201434300  
        BEGIN                                                           0000371301434400  
          IF ARRTWO[I].[31:16] NEQ CURRBLK THEN                         0000371401435000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000371501436000  
            CLOSE (CF,LOCK);                                            0000371601437000  
            CURRBLK := ARRTWO[I].[31:16];                               0000371701438000  
            FOR J := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL CURRBLK DO                          0000371801439000  
              READ(CF,300,ARRONE);                                      0000371901440000  
          END;                                                          0000372001441000  
          STRNO := ARRTWO [I].[47:16];                                  0000372101442000  
          OFF4 := ARRTWO [I].[15:16];                                   0000372201443000  
          MOREINFO:                                                     0000372301444000  
          P1 := STRFAM[STRNO,0];                                        0000372401445000  
          PACKLEN := ARRONE[OFF4+CFFILEFAMILYLOC].CFFILEFAMILYSZF;      0000372501446000  
          P2 := POINTER(ARRONE[OFF4+CFFILEFAMILYLOC])+1;                0000372601447000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY P2 FOR PACKLEN;                              0000372701448000  
          SECCIONES[STRNO] := ARRONE [OFF4+CFTOTSECTIONSLOC];           0000372801449000  
          DMPSTMP[STRNO] := ARRONE[OFF4+CFBUFFSLOC].CFDUMPSTAMPF;       0000372901450000  
          IF ARRONE[OFF4+CFTOTSECTIONSLOC] > 1 AND                      0000373001451000  
             ARRONE[OFF4+CFSECTIONNUMLOC]                               0000373101452000  
             LSS (ARRONE[OFF4+CFTOTSECTIONSLOC] - 1) THEN               0000373201453000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000373301454000  
            OFF4 := * + CFSTRRECSIZEV;                                  0000373401455000  
            IF OFF4 + CFSTRRECSIZEV > 299 THEN                          0000373501456000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000373601457000  
              CLOSE (CF,LOCK);                                          0000373701458000  
              CURRBLK := * + 1;                                         0000373801459000  
              FOR J := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL CURRBLK DO                        0000373901460000  
                READ(CF,300,ARRONE);                                    0000374001461000  
              OFF4 := 0;                                                0000374101462000  
            END;                                                        0000374201463000  
            GO MOREINFO;                                                0000374301464000  
          END;                                                          0000374401464100  
        END;                                                            0000374501465000  
      END;                                                              0000374601466000  
    END;                                                                0000374701467000  
                                                                        0000374801468000  
    PROCEDURE READDESCRIPCION;                                          0000374901469000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000375001470000  
      OPEN (DESCRIPCION);                                               0000375101471000  
      LASTREC := DESCRIPCION.LASTRECORD;                                0000375201472000  
      RESIZE (DARRAY[*,*],LASTREC+1,RETAIN);                            0000375301473000  
      FOR I := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL LASTREC DO                                0000375401474000  
        READ (DESCRIPCION,256,DARRAY[I,*]);                             0000375501475000  
      BLKDIR := DARRAY[0,3].[31:16];                                    0000375601476000  
      BLKSINFILE := DARRAY[0,3].[15:16];                                0000375701477000  
      RESIZE(LOGBLKS[*,*],BLKSINFILE,RETAIN);                           0000375801478000  
      FOR I := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL BLKSINFILE DO                             0000375901479000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000376001480000  
        BLOCKNO := DARRAY[0,BLKDIR+I].[23:24];                          0000376101481000  
        IF BLOCKNO < 2048 AND BLOCKNO > 0 THEN                          0000376201482000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000376301483000  
          BLKSINREC := DARRAY[BLOCKNO,0].[15:16];                       0000376401484000  
          RESIZE(LOGBLKS[I,*],BLKSINREC*256);                           0000376501485000  
          P1 := POINTER(LOGBLKS[I,0]);                                  0000376601486000  
          FOR J := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL BLKSINREC - 1 DO                      0000376701487000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000376801488000  
            P2 := POINTER(DARRAY[BLOCKNO+J,0]);                         0000376901489000  
            REPLACE P1:P1 BY P2 FOR 256 WORDS;                          0000377001490000  
          END;                                                          0000377101491000  
        END ELSE I := BLKSINFILE + 1;                                   0000377201492000  
      END;                                                              0000377301493000  
      P1 := POINTER(DARRAY[0,0]);                                       0000377401494000  
      P2 := POINTER(LOGBLKS[0,0]);                                      0000377501495000  
      REPLACE P2:P2 BY P1:P1 FOR 256 WORDS;                             0000377601496000  
      DBLIST := LOGBLKS[0,DBNODELOC].NODELIST;                          0000377701497000  
      DBBLOCK := 1;                                                     0000377801498000  
      STRS := LOGBLKS[DBBLOCK,DBLIST].[23:24];                          0000377901499000  
      FOR STR := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL STRS DO                                 0000378001500000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000378101501000  
        BLCKNO := LOGBLKS[DBBLOCK,DBLIST+STR].[47:16];                  0000378201502000  
        BLOFF  := LOGBLKS[DBBLOCK,DBLIST+STR].[15:16];                  0000378301503000  
% CONVERT BLCKNO TO BLOCKNO VIA BLOCKDIR                                0000378401504000  
        BLOCKNO := BLCKNO;                                              0000378501505000  
        TEXTBASE := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,2];                                 0000378601506000  
        IF LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+TYPEF] = DATASET THEN                  0000378701507000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000378801508000  
          I := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+PACKNAMETEXT];                     0000378901509000  
          P := POINTER(LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,TEXTBASE+I])+1;                  0000379001510000  
          PS := STRFAM[STR,0];                                          0000379101511000  
          IF LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+NEWPLATEAU] = 1 THEN                 0000379201512000  
             EXT[STR] := TRUE;                                          0000379301513000  
          IF LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+VFNODE] > 0 THEN                     0000379401514000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000379501515000  
            VF[STR] := TRUE;                                            0000379601516000  
            VARFNODE := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+VFNODE];                  0000379701517000  
            VAR1 := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VARFNODE].[31:16];                  0000379801518000  
            VAR2 := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VAR1].[15:16];                      0000379901519000  
            FOR M := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL VAR2 DO                             0000380001520000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000380101521000  
              VFTPE := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VAR1+M].[15:16];                 0000380201522000  
              VAR3 := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VFTPE+VFTYPE];                    0000380301523000  
              VAR4 := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VFTPE+RECORDSZ];                  0000380401524000  
              VFDATA[STR,VAR3].[23:16] := VAR4;                         0000380501525000  
              VAR4 := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,VFTPE+VFTYPE];                    0000380601526000  
              VFDATA[STR,VAR3].[07:08] := VAR4;                         0000380701527000  
            END;                                                        0000380801528000  
          END;                                                          0000380901529000  
          RECTYPENO := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+RECORDTYPEITEMNUM];        0000381001530000  
          I := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+DATAITEMNODE];                     0000381101531000  
          J := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,I].[31:16];                              0000381201532000  
          K := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,J].[23:24];                              0000381301533000  
          FOR L := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL K DO                                  0000381401534000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000381501535000  
            ITEMOFF :=  LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,J+L].[15:16];                   0000381601536000  
            ITEMNO  := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,ITEMOFF+ITEMNUM];                0000381701537000  
            IF ITEMNO = RECTYPENO THEN                                  0000381801538000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000381901539000  
              RECTYPESZ[STR] := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,ITEMOFF+NUMTYPES];      0000382001540000  
            END;                                                        0000382101541000  
          END;                                                          0000382201542000  
        END                                                             0000382301543000  
        ELSE                                                            0000382401544000  
        IF LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+TYPEF] = INDEXSET THEN                 0000382501545000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000382601546000  
          SBLOCKNO := BLOCKNO;                                          0000382701547000  
          SBLOFF   := BLOFF;                                            0000382801548000  
          MSTRNUM := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+MASTERSTRUCTURESN];          0000382901549000  
          IF MSTRNUM > 1 THEN                                           0000383001550000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000383101551000  
            BLCKNO := LOGBLKS[DBBLOCK,DBLIST+MSTRNUM].[47:16];          0000383201552000  
            BLOFF  := LOGBLKS[DBBLOCK,DBLIST+MSTRNUM].[15:16];          0000383301553000  
    % CONVERT BLCKNO TO BLOCKNO VIA BLOCKDIR                            0000383401554000  
            BLOCKNO := BLCKNO;                                          0000383501555000  
            I := LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+IDENTIFIERSZ];                   0000383601556000  
            P := POINTER(LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+WORDONE])+1;             0000383701557000  
            REPLACE DATASETNAME[STR,0] BY P FOR I," " FOR 17-I;         0000383801558000  
            BLOCKNO := SBLOCKNO;                                        0000383901559000  
            BLOFF   := SBLOFF;                                          0000384001560000  
          END;                                                          0000384101561000  
          IF LOGBLKS[BLOCKNO,BLOFF+SUBSETF] = 1  THEN                   0000384201562000  
            SUBSET[STR] := TRUE;                                        0000384301563000  
        END;                                                            0000384401564000  
      END;                                                              0000384501565000  
    END;                                                                0000384601566000  
                                                                        0000384701567000  
    PROCEDURE PROCESASET;                                               0000384801568000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000384901569000  
        IF SUBSET[INT2] THEN RESULTADO := 4                             0000385001570000  
        ELSE                                                            0000385101571000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000385201572000  
          REPLACE NOMBRESET[0] BY ESTRUCTURA[0] FOR 17;                 0000385301573000  
          REPLACE ESTRUCTURA[0] BY DATASETNAME[INT2,0] FOR 17;          0000385401574000  
          SETVALIDO := TRUE;                                            0000385501575000  
        END                                                             0000385601576000  
    END;                                                                0000385701577000

    PROCEDURE MONITOR_SUPPORT (MONITOR, DBTITLE);                       0000385901579000  
      STRING                            DBTITLE ;                       0000386001580000  
      PROCEDURE                MONITOR                                  0000386101581000  
       (DBOPTIONSWORD, MAXSTRUCTURENUMBER, DMINQ);                      0000386201582000  
        VALUE DBOPTIONSWORD, MAXSTRUCTURENUMBER;                        0000386301583000  
       BOOLEAN DBOPTIONSWORD;                                           0000386401584000  
       REAL MAXSTRUCTURENUMBER;                                         0000386501585000  
       BOOLEAN PROCEDURE DMINQ (A); ARRAY A [0]; FORMAL;                0000386601586000  
       FORMAL;                                                          0000386701587000  
       LIBRARY DMSUPPORT;                                               0000386801588000
