    PROCEDURE MONITOR(DBOPT,MAXSTR,DMINQ);                              0000387001590000  
      VALUE DBOPT, MAXSTR;                                              0000387101591000  
      BOOLEAN DBOPT;                                                    0000387201592000  
      REAL MAXSTR;                                                      0000387301593000  
      BOOLEAN PROCEDURE DMINQ (A); ARRAY A [0]; FORMAL;                 0000387401594000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000387501595000  
      PROCEDURE STDFF;                                                  0000387601596000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000387701597000  
        RPB := DMA[STARTADDR+8];                                        0000387801598000  
        BS1 := DMA[STARTADDR+9];                                        0000387901599000  
        SEC := BS1 DIV 30;                                              0000388001600000  
        RES := BS1 MOD 30;                                              0000388101601000  
        IF RES > 0 THEN SEC := * + 1;                                   0000388201602000  
        BS2 := SEC * 30;                                                0000388301603000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+11] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000388401604000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+12] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000388501605000  
        IF DMPSTMP[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 0 THEN BS1 := * - 1;             0000388601606000  
        RECSZ := (BS1/RPB);                                             0000388701607000  
        POBLACION := 0;                                                 0000388801608000  
        FOR J := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] DO          0000388901609000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000389001610000  
          P1 := STRNAME[0];                                             0000389101611000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY BASE[0] UNTIL = " ","/",                     0000389201612000  
                           ESTRUCTURA FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA,            0000389301613000  
                               "/DATA";                                 0000389401614000  
          IF SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 1 THEN                       0000389501615000  
            REPLACE P1:P1 BY "/",J-1 FOR * DIGITS;                      0000389601616000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY " ON ", STRFAM[DMA[STARTADDR+1],0]           0000389701617000  
                           FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA;                       0000389801618000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY ".";                                         0000389901619000  
%         DISPLAY (STRNAME);                                            0000390001620000  
          REPLACE DF.TITLE BY STRNAME[0];                               0000390101621000  
          OPEN (DF);                                                    0000390201622000  
          READ(DF[DF.LASTRECORD],30,DATOS)[EV1];                        0000390301623000  
          WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                            0000390401624000  
          PROCIORESULTADO;                                              0000390501625000  
          TOTALWORDS := DATOS[1].[47:28]*30;                            0000390601626000  
          BLOCKS := TOTALWORDS/BS2;                                     0000390701627000  
          RECS1 := (BLOCKS * RPB) - 1;                                  0000390801628000  
          BAF := DATOS[1].[15:16];                                      0000390901629000  
          IF BAF > 0 THEN                                               0000391001630000  
            RECS2 := BAF/RECSZ                                          0000391101631000  
          ELSE RECS2 := 0;                                              0000391201632000  
          TOTALRECS := RECS1 + RECS2;                                   0000391301633000  
          DKNUM := DATOS[0];                                            0000391401634000  
          DKBASE := DATOS[2];                                           0000391501635000  
          IF DATOS[3] NEQ 0 THEN                                        0000391601636000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000391701637000  
            READ(DF[DKBASE+DKNUM+1],30,DATOS1)[EV1];                    0000391801638000  
            WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                          0000391901639000  
            PROCIORESULTADO;                                            0000392001640000  
            DKRECS := (DKNUM*29) + DATOS1[0];                           0000392101641000  
          END                                                           0000392201642000  
          ELSE DKRECS := 0;                                             0000392301643000  
          CLOSE(DF);                                                    0000392401644000  
          POBLACION :=  * + (TOTALRECS - DKRECS);                       0000392501645000  
        END;                                                            0000392601646000  
      END;                                                              0000392701647000  
                                                                        0000392801648000  
      PROCEDURE STDVF;                                                  0000392901649000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000393001650000  
        RPB := DMA[STARTADDR+8];                                        0000393101651000  
        BS1 := DMA[STARTADDR+9];                                        0000393201652000  
        SEC := BS1 DIV 30;                                              0000393301653000  
        RES := BS1 MOD 30;                                              0000393401654000  
        IF RES > 0 THEN SEC := * + 1;                                   0000393501655000  
        BS2 := SEC * 30;                                                0000393601656000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+11] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000393701657000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+12] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000393801658000  
        IF DMPSTMP[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 0 THEN BS1 := * - 1;             0000393901659000  
        BS1 := * - 1; % CONTROL WORD                                    0000394001660000  
        RECSZ := (BS1/RPB);                                             0000394101661000  
        FOR J := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] DO          0000394201662000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000394301663000  
          P1 := STRNAME[0];                                             0000394401664000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY BASE[0] UNTIL = " ","/",                     0000394501665000  
                           ESTRUCTURA FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA,            0000394601666000  
                               "/DATA";                                 0000394701667000  
          IF SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 1 THEN                       0000394801668000  
            REPLACE P1:P1 BY "/",J-1 FOR * DIGITS;                      0000394901669000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY " ON ", STRFAM[DMA[STARTADDR+1],0]           0000395001670000  
                           FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA;                       0000395101671000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY ".";                                         0000395201672000  
%         DISPLAY (STRNAME);                                            0000395301673000  
          REPLACE DF.TITLE BY STRNAME[0];                               0000395401674000  
          OPEN (DF);                                                    0000395501675000  
          LASTREC := DF.LASTRECORD + 1;                                 0000395601676000  
          RESIZE(BUFF,BS2,DISCARD);                                     0000395701677000  
          READ(DF[0],BS2,BUFF)[EV1];                                    0000395801678000  
          WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                            0000395901679000  
          PROCIORESULTADO;                                              0000396001680000  
          FOR INT := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL RECTYPESZ[INT2]+1 DO                0000396101681000  
            IF VFDATA[INT2,INT].[07:08] > 0 THEN J1 := * + 1;           0000396201682000  
          J1 := * + 1; % ADD RECORDTYPE 0                               0000396301683000  
          VFDATA[INT2,0].[23:16] := RECSZ;                              0000396401684000  
          IF J = 1 THEN                                                 0000396501685000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000396601686000  
            TIPOS := RECTYPESZ[INT2];                                   0000396701687000  
            FOR INT3 := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL TIPOS DO                         0000396801688000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000396901689000  
              INT4 := VFDATA[INT2,INT3].[23:16];                        0000397001690000  
              INT4 := * + RECSZ;                                        0000397101691000  
              VFDATA[INT2,INT3].[23:16] := INT4;                        0000397201692000  
            END;                                                        0000397301693000  
            IF RECTYPESZ[INT2] > 127 THEN TYPESZ := 2                   0000397401694000  
                                     ELSE TYPESZ := 1;                  0000397501695000  
          END;                                                          0000397601696000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000397701697000  
            AREALEN := MIN(65520,DF.AREALENGTH);                        0000397801698000  
%           J3 := LASTREC*30;                                           0000397901699000  
            J3 := ENTIER(LASTREC*30 / AREALEN);                         0000398001700000  
            IF LASTREC*30 NEQ AREALEN THEN J3 := * + 1;                 0000398101700100  
            CLOSE(DF);                                                  0000398201701000  
            OPEN(DF);                                                   0000398301702000  
            FOR N := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL J3 DO                               0000398401703000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000398501704000  
              RESIZE (BUFF,AREALEN,DISCARD);                            0000398601705000  
              READ(DF,AREALEN,BUFF)[EV1];                               0000398701706000  
              WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                        0000398801707000  
              PROCIORESULTADO;                                          0000398901708000  
              FOR J4 := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL (AREALEN/BS2)-1 DO               0000399001709000  
              BEGIN                                                     0000399101710000  
                REPLACE POINTER(BUFF1[0]) BY                            0000399201711000  
                  POINTER(BUFF[J4*BS2]) FOR BS2 WORDS;                  0000399301712000  
                IF BUFF1[0].[44:01] = 1 THEN                            0000399401713000  
                BEGIN                                                   0000399501714000  
                  BASEREC := 1;                                         0000399601715000  
                  IF EXT[INT2] THEN                                     0000399701716000  
                    EXTOFF := 2                                         0000399801717000  
                  ELSE                                                  0000399901718000  
                    EXTOFF := 0;                                        0000400001719000  
                  WHILE BASEREC + EXTOFF < BUFF1[0].[43:16] DO          0000400101720000  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000400201721000  
                    INT3 := BUFF1[BASEREC+EXTOFF].[47:04];              0000400301722000  
                    IF TYPESZ = 2 THEN                                  0000400401723000  
                    BEGIN                                               0000400501724000  
                      J1 := BUFF1[BASEREC+EXTOFF].[43:04];              0000400601725000  
                      INT3 := (INT3*16)+J1;                             0000400701726000  
                    END;                                                0000400801727000  
                    IF BUFF1[BASEREC+EXTOFF].[39:04] NEQ 4"F" THEN      0000400901728000  
                       POBLACION := * + 1;                              0000401001729000  
                    BASEREC := * + VFDATA[INT2,INT3].[23:16];           0000401101730000  
                  END                                                   0000401201731000  
                END;                                                    0000401301732000  
              END;                                                      0000401401733000  
            END;                                                        0000401501734000  
          END;                                                          0000401601735000  
          CLOSE (DF);                                                   0000401701736000  
        END;                                                            0000401801737000  
      END;                                                              0000401901738000  
                                                                        0000402001739000  
      PROCEDURE DIRECTO;                                                0000402101740000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000402201741000  
        RPB := DMA[STARTADDR+8];                                        0000402301742000  
        BS1 := DMA[STARTADDR+9];                                        0000402401743000  
        SEC := BS1 DIV 30;                                              0000402501744000  
        RES := BS1 MOD 30;                                              0000402601745000  
        IF RES > 0 THEN SEC := * + 1;                                   0000402701746000  
        BS2 := SEC * 30;                                                0000402801747000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+11] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000402901748000  
        IF DMA[STARTADDR+12] = 1 THEN BS1 := * - 1;                     0000403001749000  
        IF DMPSTMP[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 0 THEN BS1 := * - 1;             0000403101750000  
        RECSZ := (BS1/RPB);                                             0000403201751000  
        POBLACION := 0;                                                 0000403301752000  
        FOR INT := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] DO        0000403401753000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000403501754000  
          P1 := STRNAME[0];                                             0000403601755000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY BASE[0] UNTIL = " ","/",                     0000403701756000  
                           ESTRUCTURA FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA,            0000403801757000  
                               "/DATA";                                 0000403901758000  
          IF SECCIONES[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] > 1 THEN                       0000404001759000  
            REPLACE P1:P1 BY "/",INT-1 FOR * DIGITS;                    0000404101760000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY " ON ", STRFAM[DMA[STARTADDR+1],0]           0000404201761000  
                           FOR 17 WHILE IN ALPHA;                       0000404301762000  
          REPLACE P1:P1 BY ".";                                         0000404401763000  
%         DISPLAY (STRNAME);                                            0000404501764000  
          REPLACE DF.TITLE BY STRNAME[0];                               0000404601765000  
          OPEN (DF);                                                    0000404701766000  
          LASTREC := DF.LASTRECORD + 1;                                 0000404801767000  
          IF LASTREC > 0 THEN                                           0000404901768000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000405001769000  
            RESIZE (BUFF,30,DISCARD);                                   0000405101770000  
            READ(DF,30,BUFF)[EV1];                                      0000405201771000  
            WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                          0000405301772000  
            PROCIORESULTADO;                                            0000405401773000  
            P1 := POINTER(BUFF[0],4);                                   0000405501774000  
            SCAN P1:P1 UNTIL = 4"F";                                    0000405601775000  
            KEYST := OFFSET(P1);                                        0000405701776000  
            SCAN P1:P1 WHILE = 4"F";                                    0000405801777000  
            KEYEND := OFFSET(P1);                                       0000405901778000  
            KEYLEN := KEYEND-KEYST;                                     0000406001779000  
            CLOSE(DF);                                                  0000406101780000  
            OPEN(DF);                                                   0000406201781000  
            IF EXT[DMA[STARTADDR+1]] THEN                               0000406301782000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000406401783000  
              RESIZE (BUFF,BS2,DISCARD);                                0000406501784000  
              READ(DF,BS2,BUFF)[EV1];                                   0000406601785000  
              WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                        0000406701786000  
              PROCIORESULTADO;                                          0000406801787000  
              TOTALRECS:= INTEGER(BUFF[2]);                             0000406901788000  
            END                                                         0000407001789000  
            ELSE                                                        0000407101790000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000407201791000  
              RECS1 := 0;                                               0000407301792000  
              AREALEN := MIN(65520,DF.AREALENGTH);                      0000407401793000  
%             J1 := LASTREC*30;                                         0000407501794000  
              J1 := ENTIER(LASTREC*30 / AREALEN);                       0000407601795000  
              IF LASTREC*30 NEQ AREALEN THEN J1 := * + 1;               0000407701795100  
              FOR N := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL J1 DO                             0000407801796000  
              BEGIN                                                     0000407901797000  
                RESIZE (BUFF,AREALEN,DISCARD);                          0000408001798000  
                READ(DF,AREALEN,BUFF)[EV1];                             0000408101799000  
                WAITANDRESET(EV1);                                      0000408201800000  
                PROCIORESULTADO;                                        0000408301801000  
                FOR J2 := 0 STEP 1 UNTIL (AREALEN/BS2)-1 DO             0000408401802000  
                BEGIN                                                   0000408501803000  
                REPLACE POINTER(BUFF1[0]) BY                            0000408601804000  
                  POINTER(BUFF[J2*BS2]) FOR BS2 WORDS;                  0000408701805000  
                P1 := POINTER(BUFF1[0],4);                              0000408801806000  
                P1 := * + (KEYST );                                     0000408901807000  
                FOR INT3 := 1 STEP 1 UNTIL RPB DO                       0000409001808000  
                BEGIN                                                   0000409101809000  
                  INT4 := INTEGER(P1,KEYLEN);                           0000409201810000  
                  IF INT4 = RECS1 AND INT4 NEQ 0 THEN                   0000409301811000  
                    TOTALRECS := * + 1;                                 0000409401812000  
                  RECS1:= * + 1;                                        0000409501813000  
                  P1 := * + ((RECSZ*12));                               0000409601814000  
                END;                                                    0000409701815000  
                END;                                                    0000409801816000  
              END;                                                      0000409901817000  
            END;                                                        0000410001818000  
          END                                                           0000410101819000  
          ELSE                                                          0000410201820000  
            TOTALRECS := 0;                                             0000410301821000  
          CLOSE(DF);                                                    0000410401822000  
          POBLACION :=  * +  TOTALRECS;                                 0000410501823000  
        END;                                                            0000410601824000  
      END;                                                              0000410701825000  
                                                                        0000410801826000  
      FOR INT2:= 1 STEP 1 UNTIL MAXSTR DO                               0000410901827000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000411001828000  
        DMA[0] := 25;                                                   0000411101829000  
        DMA[1] := INT2;                                                 0000411201830000  
        DMA[2] := 0;                                                    0000411301831000  
        DMINQ (DMA);                                                    0000411401832000  
        IF DMA[3] = 0 THEN                                              0000411501833000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000411601834000  
          STARTADDR := DMA[5];                                          0000411701835000  
          SG := DMA[STARTADDR].[23:08];                                 0000411801836000  
          LEN  := DMA[STARTADDR+2].[47:08];                             0000411901837000  
          P := POINTER(DMA[STARTADDR+2])+1;                             0000412001838000  
          SCAN ESTRUCTURA[0] FOR J:17 WHILE IN ALPHA;                   0000412101839000  
          IF LEN = 17-J AND                                             0000412201840000  
            P = ESTRUCTURA FOR LEN THEN                                 0000412301841000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000412401842000  
            ENCONTROSTR := TRUE;                                        0000412501843000  
            IF DMA[STARTADDR+5] = 2 AND                                 0000412601844000  
               DMA[STARTADDR+6] = 0 THEN                                0000412701845000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000412801846000  
              IF NOT VF[INT2] THEN                                      0000412901847000  
                STDFF ELSE                                              0000413001848000  
                STDVF                                                   0000413101849000  
            END                                                         0000413201850000  
            ELSE                                                        0000413301851000  
            IF DMA[STARTADDR+5] = 2 AND                                 0000413401852000  
               DMA[STARTADDR+6] = 5 THEN                                0000413501853000  
            DIRECTO                                                     0000413601854000  
            ELSE                                                        0000413701855000  
            IF DMA[STARTADDR+5] = 5 THEN                                0000413801856000  
               PROCESASET                                               0000413901857000  
            ELSE                                                        0000414001858000  
              RESULTADO := 2;                                           0000414101859000  
          END;                                                          0000414201860000  
        END;                                                            0000414301861000  
      END;                                                              0000414401862000  
    END;                                                                0000414501863000  
                                                                        0000414601864000  
    ON ANYFAULT [MENS[0]:ERR]:                                          0000414701865000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000414801866000  
        RESULTADO := 5;                                                 0000414901867000  
        POBLACION := 0;                                                 0000415001868000  
        REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "ERROR ", ERR FOR 3 DIGITS,":",             0000415101869000  
                            MENS[0] FOR 100;                            0000415201870000  
%       DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                   0000415301870100

      END;                                                              0000415901873000

    P1 := CFNAME[0];                                                    0000416101875000  
                                                                        0000416201876000

    IF NOT CF.RESIDENT THEN                                             0000417301887000  
      RESULTADO := 1                                                    0000417401888000  
    ELSE                                                                0000417501889000

                       "DESCRIPTION/",                                  0000418401898000  
                       P2:P2 FOR I-1,".";                               0000418501899000

%     DISPLAY(DESCNAME[0]);                                             0000418701901000

      IF NOT DESCRIPCION.RESIDENT THEN                                  0000419001904000  
        RESULTADO := 3                                                  0000419101905000  
      ELSE                                                              0000419201906000

        S := STRING(BASE[0],57);                                        0000419601910000

        SETVALIDO := FALSE;                                             0000419801912000

        BEGIN                                                           0000420501919000  
           MONITOR_SUPPORT(MONITOR,S);                                  0000420601920000

 INTEGER PROCEDURE S038L035_30_USERXCSI_C(EA_ENTRADA,EA_SALIDA);        0000422101935000

%********************************************************************** 0000422301937000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000422401938000  
   INTEGER  I_RESULT                                                    0000422501939000  
           ,I_CUANTOS                                                   0000422601940000  
           ,I_USER                                                      0000422701941000  
           ,I_IND                                                       0000422801942000

           ,I_IND_S                                                     0000423001944000  
           ;                                                            0000423101945000

   BOOLEAN B_SIGUE;                                                     0000423301947000

 PROCEDURE USERXCSI;                                                    0000423501949000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000423601950000  
   IF I_CUANTOS = 0 THEN                                                0000423701951000  
      I_CUANTOS:= (SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 4;                          0000423801952000  
                                                                        0000423901953000  
   IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                   0000424001954000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000424101955000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:USERXCSI: ENTRO A DAR USER X CSI");                 0000424201956000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:USERXCSI: USER INI " !! STRING(I_USER, 6));         0000424301957000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:USERXCSI: CUANTOS " !! STRING(I_CUANTOS, 8));       0000424401958000  
   END;                                                                 0000424501959000  
   I_IND_USER:= 1;                                                      0000424601960000  
   I_IND_S   := 0;                                                      0000424701961000  
   B_SIGUE:= TRUE;                                                      0000424801962000  
   WHILE B_SIGUE DO                                                     0000424901963000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000425001964000  
     IF I_IND_S < I_CUANTOS AND I_IND_USER < 1000 THEN                  0000425101965000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000425201966000  
       IF LA_USERS[I_IND_USER] = 1 AND                                  0000425301967000  
          I_IND_USER > I_USER      THEN                                 0000425401968000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000425501969000  
         REPLACE DF_UXC_ARR_USER_S(I_IND_S) BY I_IND_USER FOR 4 DIGITS; 0000425601970000  
         IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                             0000425701971000  
         BEGIN                                                          0000425801972000  
           DISPLAY ("L035:USERXCSI: ENCONTRE USER, USER " !!            0000425901973000  
                    STRING(I_IND_USER, 6)!!                             0000426001974000  
                    " GRABO EN OCURRENCIA " !!                          0000426101975000  
                    STRING(I_IND_S, 6));                                0000426201976000  
         END;                                                           0000426301977000  
         I_IND_S:= * + 1;                                               0000426401978000  
       END;                                                             0000426501979000  
       I_IND_USER:= * + 1;                                              0000426601980000  
     END                                                                0000426701981000  
     ELSE                                                               0000426801982000  
       B_SIGUE:= FALSE;                                                 0000426901983000  
   END;                                                                 0000427001984000  
                                                                        0000427101985000  
   REPLACE DF_UXC_NUM_USER_S BY I_IND_S FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000427201986000  
                                                                        0000427301987000  
   IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                   0000427401988000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000427501989000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:USERXCSI: NUM USERS " !!                            0000427601990000  
              STRING(I_IND_S,6));                                       0000427701991000  
   END;                                                                 0000427801992000  
                                                                        0000427901993000  
                                                                        0000428001994000  
 END;                                                                   0000428101995000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000428201996000  
% OUTER BLOCK S038L035_30_USERXCSI_C                                    0000428301997000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000428401998000  
  REPLACE EA_SALIDA BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_SALIDA);                      0000428501999000  
                                                                        0000428602000000  
  IF SIZE(EA_ENTRADA) < 6 THEN                                          0000428702001000  
     I_RESULT:= 17;                                                     0000428802002000  
                                                                        0000428902003000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000429002004000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000429102005000  
    I_USER   := IDF_UXC_USER_E;                                         0000429202006000  
    I_CUANTOS:= IDF_UXC_CUANTOS_E;                                      0000429302007000  
                                                                        0000429402008000  
    IF I_CUANTOS > ((SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 4) THEN                   0000429502009000  
       I_RESULT:= 9;                                                    0000429602010000  
  END;                                                                  0000429702011000  
                                                                        0000429802012000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000429902013000  
     USERXCSI;                                                          0000430002014000  
                                                                        0000430102015000  
  S038L035_30_USERXCSI_C:= I_RESULT;                                    0000430202016000  
                                                                        0000430302017000  
 END OF S038L035_30_USERXCSI_C;                                         0000430402018000

 BEGIN                                                                  0000432302037000

      IF I_CUANTOS = 0 THEN                                             0000432502039000  
         I_CUANTOS:= (SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 42;                      0000432602040000

      BEGIN                                                             0000433902053000

             DF_AXU_NUM_TPO_PROC(I_USER,I_AGRUP) > I_NUM_AGRUP THEN     0000434902063000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000435002064000  
           IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                           0000435102065000

                      " GRABA EN OCURR: " !!                            0000435502069000

             DISPLAY (E_SCR_DSP);                                       0000436002074000

            DF_AXU_NUM_TPO_PROC(I_USER,I_AGRUP) FOR  2 DIGITS;          0000436302077000  
           REPLACE DF_AXU_NOM_AGRUP_S(I_IND_S) BY                       0000436402078000  
            DF_AXU_NOM_TPO_PROC(I_USER,I_AGRUP) FOR 40;                 0000436502079000  
           I_IND_S:= * + 1;                                             0000436602080000  
          END;                                                          0000436702081000  
          I_AGRUP:= * + 1;                                              0000436802082000

        ELSE                                                            0000437002084000

                                                                        0000437402088000  
 END;                                                                   0000437502089000

 INTEGER PROCEDURE S038L035_32_PROGSXAGRUP_C(EA_ENTRADA,EA_SALIDA);     0000441202126000  
 EBCDIC ARRAY EA_ENTRADA[0], EA_SALIDA[0];                              0000441302127000  
%********************************************************************** 0000441402128000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000441502129000  
   INTEGER  I_RESULT                                                    0000441602130000  
           ,I_CUANTOS                                                   0000441702131000  
           ,I_USER_CORRIDA                                              0000441802132000  
           ,I_NUM_PGM                                                   0000441902133000  
           ,I_NUM_COPIA                                                 0000442002134000  
           ,I_TIPO_PGM                                                  0000442102135000  
           ,I_NUM_AGRUP                                                 0000442202136000  
           ,I_T_TIPO_PGM                                                0000442302137000  
           ,I_T_PGM                                                     0000442402138000  
           ,I_T_COPIA                                                   0000442502139000  
           ,I_IND_PGM                                                   0000442602140000  
           ,I_IND_S                                                     0000442702141000  
           ,I_NUM_ACTI                                                  0000442802142000  
           ,I_FECH_TER                                                  0000442902143000  
           ,I_HORA_TER                                                  0000443002144000  
           ;                                                            0000443102145000  
                                                                        0000443202146000  
   DOUBLE D_TEM_HORA                                                    0000443302147000  
         ,D_ET_TOTAL                                                    0000443402148000  
         ,D_ET_ACTUAL                                                   0000443502149000  
         ,D_ET_FALTANTE                                                 0000443602150000  
         ,D_ET                                                          0000443702150100  
         ;                                                              0000443802151000  
                                                                        0000443902152000  
 PROCEDURE DATOSCOMUNES;                                                0000444002153000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000444102154000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_USER_S     (I_IND_S) BY                               0000444202155000  
           DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA(I_IND_PGM) FOR 4 DIGITS;                  0000444302156000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_TIPO_PROC_S(I_IND_S) BY                               0000444402157000  
           DF_DP_TIPO_PGM    (I_IND_PGM) FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000444502158000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_NUM_PGM_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000444602159000  
           DF_DP_NUM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM) FOR 4 DIGITS;                  0000444702160000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_COPIA_PGM_S(I_IND_S) BY                               0000444802161000  
           DF_DP_NUM_COPIA   (I_IND_PGM) FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000444902162000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_NOM_PGM_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000445002163000  
           DF_DP_NOM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM) FOR 80;                        0000445102164000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_FEC_INI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000445202165000  
           DF_DP_FECH_INI    (I_IND_PGM) FOR 8 DIGITS;                  0000445302166000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_HRA_INI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000445402167000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                              0000445502168000  
           DF_DP_HORA_INI    (I_IND_PGM)) FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000445602169000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_FEC_FIN_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000445702170000  
           DF_DP_FECH_TER    (I_IND_PGM) FOR 8 DIGITS;                  0000445802171000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_HRA_FIN_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000445902172000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                              0000446002173000  
           DF_DP_HORA_TER    (I_IND_PGM)) FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000446102174000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_NUM_MIX_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000446202175000  
           DF_DP_NUM_MIX     (I_IND_PGM) FOR 6 DIGITS;                  0000446302176000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_DSP_INFO_S (I_IND_S) BY                               0000446402177000  
           DF_DP_TIPO_DSPINFO (I_IND_PGM) FOR 2 DIGITS;                 0000446502178000  
 END;                                                                   0000446602179000  
                                                                        0000446702180000  
 PROCEDURE PROGENPROC;                                                  0000446802181000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000446902182000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_ET_ANT_S   (I_IND_S) BY                               0000447002183000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                              0000447102184000  
                              DF_DP_ET_ANTERIOR (I_IND_PGM))            0000447202185000  
           FOR 10 DIGITS;                                               0000447302186000  
   IF DF_DP_PGM_TERMINADO(I_IND_PGM) = DF_DP_PT_ETOK THEN               0000447402187000  
      REPLACE DF_PXA_ET_ACT_S   (I_IND_S) BY                            0000447502188000  
              I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                           0000447602189000  
                                 DF_DP_ET_ACTUAL(I_IND_PGM))            0000447702190000  
              FOR 10 DIGITS                                             0000447802191000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000447902192000  
     REPLACE DF_PXA_ET_ACT_S   (I_IND_S) BY                             0000448002193000  
             I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(                                        0000448102194000  
                    D_PROC_ET(DF_DP_FECH_INI   (I_IND_PGM)              0000448202195000  
                             ,DF_DP_HORA_INI   (I_IND_PGM)              0000448302196000  
                             ,I_PROC_FECHA                              0000448402197000  
                             ,I_PROC_HORA))                             0000448502198000  
                      FOR 10 DIGITS;                                    0000448602199000  
%           REPLACE DF_PXA_AVANCE_S   (I_IND_S) BY %                    0000448702200000  
%           REPLACE DF_PXA_TE_TERM_S  (I_IND_S) BY %                    0000448802201000  
                                                                        0000448902202000  
   IF DF_DP_PGM_TERMINADO(I_IND_PGM) = DF_DP_PT_ETOK THEN               0000449002203000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000449102204000  
     REPLACE DF_PXA_TE_TERM_S  (I_IND_S) BY   0 FOR 10 DIGITS;          0000449202205000  
     REPLACE DF_PXA_AVANCE_S   (I_IND_S) BY 100 FOR  4 DIGITS;          0000449302206000  
   END                                                                  0000449402207000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000449502208000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000449602209000  
                                                                        0000449702210000  
   D_ET_FALTANTE:= 0;                                                   0000449802211000  
   D_ET_ACTUAL:= 0;                                                     0000449902212000  
                                                                        0000450002213000  
% Se calculan los tiempos en cada actividad para sacar los datos para   0000450102214000  
% el programa                                                           0000450202215000  
                                                                        0000450302216000  
   FOR I_NUM_ACTI:= 1 STEP 1 UNTIL 99 DO                                0000450402217000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000450502218000  
                                                                        0000450602219000  
     IF (DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =      % Valida          0000450702220000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                      OR  % actÀs que       0000450802221000  
         DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =      % existan         0000450902222000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_TERM                      OR                    0000451002223000  
         DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000451102224000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_NOINI                       ) AND               0000451202225000  
         DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000451302226000  
         DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                           THEN              0000451402227000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000451502228000  
                                                                        0000451602229000  
       IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                       0000451702230000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_TERM                          THEN              0000451802231000  
         D_ET_ACTUAL:= * + DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)      0000451902232000  
       ELSE                                                             0000452002233000  
       IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                       0000452102234000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          THEN              0000452202235000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000452302236000  
         I_FECH_TER:= I_PROC_FECHA;                                     0000452402237000  
         I_HORA_TER:= I_PROC_HORA;                                      0000452502238000  
         D_ET:= D_PROC_ET(DF_DA_FECH_INI(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),         0000452602238100  
                          DF_DA_HORA_INI(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),         0000452702238200  
                          I_FECH_TER                          ,         0000452802238300  
                          I_HORA_TER                          );        0000452902238400  
         IF D_ET > DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) THEN         0000453002238500  
            D_ET:= DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI);             0000453102238600  
         D_ET_ACTUAL:= * + D_ET;                                        0000453202239000  
         IF DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) > 0 THEN            0000453302244000  
         BEGIN                                                          0000453402245000  
           D_ET_FALTANTE:= * + DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) -0000453502246000  
                           D_ET;                                        0000453602247000  
         END;                                                           0000453702251000  
       END                                                              0000453802252000  
       ELSE                                                             0000453902253000  
       IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                       0000454002254000  
          DF_DA_STA_ACT_NOINI                         THEN              0000454102255000  
          D_ET_FALTANTE:= * + DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_IND_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI);  0000454202256000  
                                                                        0000454302257000  
     END;                                                               0000454402258000  
   END;                                                                 0000454502259000  
                                                                        0000454602260000  
   D_ET_TOTAL:= D_ET_ACTUAL + D_ET_FALTANTE ;                           0000454702261000  
                                                                        0000454802262000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_TE_TERM_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000454902263000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:= D_ET_FALTANTE)               0000455002264000  
           FOR 10 DIGITS;                                               0000455102265000  
   IF D_ET_TOTAL > 0 THEN                                               0000455202266000  
      REPLACE DF_PXA_AVANCE_S   (I_IND_S) BY                            0000455302267000  
              ( D_ET_ACTUAL / D_ET_TOTAL * 100 ) FOR 4 DIGITS           0000455402268000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000455502269000  
      REPLACE DF_PXA_AVANCE_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 4 DIGITS;            0000455602270000  
                                                                        0000455702271000  
   END;                                                                 0000455802272000  
                                                                        0000455902273000  
 END;                                                                   0000456002274000  
                                                                        0000456102275000  
 PROCEDURE PROGTERM;                                                    0000456202276000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000456302277000  
                                                                        0000456402278000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_ET_ANT_S   (I_IND_S) BY                               0000456502279000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                              0000456602280000  
                              DF_DP_ET_ACTUAL(I_IND_PGM))               0000456702281000  
           FOR 10 DIGITS;                                               0000456802282000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_ET_ACT_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;              0000456902283000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_TE_TERM_S  (I_IND_S) BY                               0000457002284000  
           I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                              0000457102285000  
                              DF_DP_ET_ACTUAL(I_IND_PGM))               0000457202286000  
           FOR 10 DIGITS;                                               0000457302287000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_AVANCE_S   (I_IND_S) BY   0 FOR  4 DIGITS;            0000457402288000  
                                                                        0000457502289000  
 END;                                                                   0000457602290000  
                                                                        0000457702291000  
 PROCEDURE PROGSXAGRUP;                                                 0000457802292000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000457902293000  
                                                                        0000458002294000  
   IF I_NUM_PGM = 0 THEN                                                0000458102295000  
      I_NUM_PGM:= 1;                                                    0000458202296000  
   IF I_TIPO_PGM = 0 THEN                                               0000458302297000  
      I_TIPO_PGM:= 1;                                                   0000458402298000  
   IF I_CUANTOS = 0 THEN                                                0000458502299000  
      I_CUANTOS:= (SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 166;   % 166 = Tamaùo ocurr 0000458602300000  
                                                                        0000458702301000  
   IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                   0000458802302000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000458902303000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: ENTRO A DAR PROGSXAGRUP");             0000459002304000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_USER_CORRIDA " !!                    0000459102305000  
              STRING(I_USER_CORRIDA,6));                                0000459202306000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_NUM_PGM " !!                         0000459302307000  
              STRING(I_NUM_PGM,6));                                     0000459402308000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_NUM_COPIA " !!                       0000459502309000  
              STRING(I_NUM_COPIA,6));                                   0000459602310000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_TIPO_PGM " !!                        0000459702311000  
              STRING(I_TIPO_PGM,6));                                    0000459802312000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_CUANTOS " !!                         0000459902313000  
              STRING(I_CUANTOS,6));                                     0000460002314000  
     DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: I_NUM_AGRUP " !!                       0000460102315000  
              STRING(I_NUM_AGRUP,6));                                   0000460202316000  
   END;                                                                 0000460302317000  
                                                                        0000460402318000  
  I_T_TIPO_PGM:= I_TIPO_PGM;                                            0000460502319000  
  I_T_PGM     := I_NUM_PGM;                                             0000460602320000  
  I_T_COPIA   := I_NUM_COPIA + 1;                                       0000460702321000  
  I_IND_S:= 0;                                                          0000460802322000  
                                                                        0000460902323000  
   WHILE I_T_TIPO_PGM LEQ 2 DO                                          0000461002324000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000461102325000  
     WHILE I_T_PGM LEQ 1000 DO                                          0000461202326000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000461302327000  
        WHILE I_T_COPIA LEQ 15 DO                                       00004614          
        BEGIN                                                           0000461502329000  
          IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                            0000461602330000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000461702331000  
            DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: PGM : " !!                      0000461802332000  
                     STRING(I_T_PGM, 6 ));                              0000461902333000  
            DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: COPIA : " !!                    0000462002334000  
                     STRING(I_T_COPIA, 6 ));                            0000462102335000  
            DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: TIPO PGM : " !!                 0000462202336000  
                     STRING(I_T_TIPO_PGM, 6 ));                         0000462302337000  
            DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: EN ARRREGLO: " !!               0000462402338000  
                     STRING(I_IND_S, 6 ));                              0000462502339000  
          END;                                                          0000462602340000  
         IF I_IND_PGM:= LA_PROGXAGRUP[I_USER_CORRIDA,                   0000462702341000  
                                      I_NUM_AGRUP,                      0000462802342000  
                                      I_T_PGM,                          0000462902343000  
                                      I_T_COPIA,                        0000463002344000  
                                      I_T_TIPO_PGM] > 0 THEN            0000463102345000  
         BEGIN                                                          0000463202346000  
            IF I_IND_S < I_CUANTOS THEN                                 0000463302347000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000463402348000  
            IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                          0000463502349000  
            BEGIN                                                       0000463602350000  
              DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: IND_S : " !!                  0000463702351000  
                        STRING(I_IND_S, 6 ));                           0000463802352000  
              DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: IND_PGM : " !!                0000463902353000  
                        STRING(I_IND_PGM, 6 ));                         0000464002354000  
            END;                                                        0000464102355000  
                                                                        0000464202356000  
            DATOSCOMUNES;                                               0000464302357000  
                                                                        0000464402358000  
            IF DF_DP_NUM_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM)                      =     0000464502359000  
               DF_AXU_NUM_CORRIDA(I_USER_CORRIDA,                       0000464602360000  
                                  DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)) OR    0000464702361000  
               DF_AXU_NUM_CORRIDA(I_USER_CORRIDA,                       0000464802362000  
                                  DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)) =     0000464902363000  
               0                                                  OR    0000465002364000  
               DF_DP_TIPO_DSPINFO (I_IND_PGM) =                         0000465102365000  
                                  DF_DP_TPO_DSPINF_LIB            THEN  0000465202366000  
               PROGENPROC                                               0000465302367000  
            ELSE                                                        0000465402368000  
               PROGTERM;                                                0000465502369000  
                                                                        0000465602370000  
            I_IND_S:= * + 1;                                            0000465702371000  
          END                                                           0000465802372000  
          ELSE                                                          0000465902373000  
          BEGIN                                                         0000466002374000  
            I_T_TIPO_PGM:= 3;         % Se lleno el arreglo, salir ciclo0000466102375000  
            I_T_PGM     := 1001;                                        0000466202376000  
            I_T_COPIA   := 101;                                         0000466302377000  
            IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                          0000466402378000  
              DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: SALGO POR QUE SE LLENO " !!   0000466502379000  
                       " EL ARREGLO");                                  0000466602380000  
          END;                                                          0000466702381000  
         END;                                                           0000466802382000  
         IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                             0000466902383000  
           DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: INCREMENTO COPIA : " !!          0000467002384000  
                    STRING(I_T_COPIA,6));                               0000467102385000  
         I_T_COPIA:= * + 1;                                             0000467202386000  
        END;                                                            0000467302387000  
        IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                              0000467402388000  
          DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: INCREMENTO PGM : " !!             0000467502389000  
                   STRING(I_T_PGM,6));                                  0000467602390000  
        I_T_COPIA:= 1;                                                  0000467702391000  
        I_T_PGM:= * + 1;                                                0000467802392000  
     END;                                                               0000467902393000  
     IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                 0000468002394000  
       DISPLAY ("L035:PROGSXAGRUP: INCREMENTO TIPO PGM : " !!           0000468102395000  
                STRING(I_T_TIPO_PGM,6));                                0000468202396000  
     I_T_COPIA:= 1;                                                     0000468302397000  
     I_T_PGM:= 1;                                                       0000468402398000  
     I_T_TIPO_PGM:= * + 1;                                              0000468502399000  
   END;                                                                 0000468602400000  
                                                                        0000468702401000  
   REPLACE DF_PXA_NUM_PROGS_S BY I_IND_S FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000468802402000  
                                                                        0000468902403000  
 END;                                                                   0000469002404000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000469102405000  
% OUTER BLOCK S038L035_32_PROGSXAGRUP_C                                 0000469202406000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000469302407000  
  REPLACE EA_SALIDA BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_SALIDA);                      0000469402408000  
                                                                        0000469502409000  
  IF SIZE(EA_ENTRADA) < 16 THEN                                         0000469602410000  
     I_RESULT:= 17;                                                     0000469702411000  
                                                                        0000469802412000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000469902413000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000470002414000  
    I_USER_CORRIDA:= IDF_PXA_USER_E;                                    0000470102415000  
    I_NUM_PGM     := IDF_PXA_PROG_E;                                    0000470202416000  
    I_NUM_COPIA   := IDF_PXA_NUM_COPIA_E;                               0000470302417000  
    I_TIPO_PGM    := IDF_PXA_TIPO_PROC_E;                               0000470402418000  
    I_CUANTOS     := IDF_PXA_CUANTOS_E;                                 0000470502419000  
    I_NUM_AGRUP   := IDF_PXA_NUM_AGRUP_E;                               0000470602420000  
                                                                        0000470702421000  
    IF I_CUANTOS > ((SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 166) THEN                 0000470802422000  
       I_RESULT:= 9;                                                    0000470902423000  
  END;                                                                  0000471002424000  
                                                                        0000471102425000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000471202426000  
     IF I_USER_CORRIDA < 1   OR                                         0000471302427000  
        I_USER_CORRIDA > 1000 THEN                                      0000471402428000  
        I_RESULT:= 10;                                                  0000471502429000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000471602430000  
     IF I_NUM_PGM > 1000 THEN                                           0000471702431000  
        I_RESULT:= 11;                                                  0000471802432000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000471902433000  
     IF I_NUM_COPIA > 100 THEN                                          0000472002434000  
        I_RESULT:= 1;                                                   0000472102435000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000472202436000  
     IF I_TIPO_PGM > 2 THEN                                             0000472302437000  
        I_RESULT:= 2;                                                   0000472402438000  
  IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000472502439000  
     IF I_NUM_AGRUP < 1  OR                                             0000472602440000  
        I_NUM_AGRUP > 99 THEN                                           0000472702441000  
        I_RESULT:= 12;                                                  0000472802442000  
                                                                        0000472902443000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000473002444000  
      PROGSXAGRUP;                                                      0000473102445000  
                                                                        0000473202446000  
   S038L035_32_PROGSXAGRUP_C:= I_RESULT;                                0000473302447000  
                                                                        0000473402448000  
  END OF S038L035_32_PROGSXAGRUP_C;                                     0000473502449000

 INTEGER PROCEDURE S038L035_33_ACTIXPROG_C(EA_ENTRADA,EA_SALIDA);       0000473802452000  
 EBCDIC ARRAY EA_ENTRADA[0], EA_SALIDA[0];                              0000473902453000  
%********************************************************************** 0000474002454000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000474102455000  
                                                                        0000474202456000  
   INTEGER I_RESULT                                                     0000474302457000  
          ,I_USER_CORRIDA                                               0000474402458000  
          ,I_NUM_PGM                                                    0000474502459000  
          ,I_NUM_COPIA                                                  0000474602460000  
          ,I_TIPO_PGM                                                   0000474702461000  
          ,I_CUANTOS                                                    0000474802462000  
%         ,I_TIPO_ACTI                                                  0000474902463000  
          ,I_NUM_ACTI                                                   0000475002464000  
          ,I_INDICE_PGM                                                 0000475102465000  
          ,I_IND_S                                                      0000475202466000  
          ,I_PJEACT                                                     0000475302467000  
          ,I_FECH_TER                                                   0000475402468000  
          ,I_HORA_TER                                                   0000475502469000  
          ;                                                             0000475602470000  
                                                                        0000475702471000  
   BOOLEAN B_SIGUE;                                                     0000475802472000  
                                                                        0000475902473000  
   DOUBLE  D_TEM_HORA                                                   0000476002474000  
          ,D_ET_ACTUAL                                                  0000476102475000  
          ;                                                             0000476202476000  
                                                                        0000476302477000  
 PROCEDURE PROGENPROC;                                                  0000476402478000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000476502479000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_NUM_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY I_NUM_ACTI FOR 2 DIGITS;     0000476602480000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_STA_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000476702481000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACTIV    (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR 2 DIGITS;        0000476802482000  
   IF DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000476902483000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                  0000477002484000  
      REPLACE DF_AXP_PJE_ACTI_S (I_IND_S) BY                            0000477102485000  
            (I_PJEACT:=                                                 0000477202486000  
             I_PROC_PJEACT(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 4 DIGITS       0000477302487000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000477402488000  
      REPLACE DF_AXP_PJE_ACTI_S (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 4 DIGITS;            0000477502489000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_NOM_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000477602490000  
      DF_DA_NOM_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR 40;                  0000477702491000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_NUM_LLAMA2_S(I_IND_S) BY                              0000477802492000  
      DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR 10 DIGITS;        0000477902493000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_TPO_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000478002494000  
      DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV   (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR  2 DIGITS;        0000478102495000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_FEC_INI_S   (I_IND_S) BY                              0000478202496000  
      DF_DA_FECH_INI    (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR  8 DIGITS;        0000478302497000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_HRA_INI_S   (I_IND_S) BY                              0000478402498000  
      I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                                   0000478502499000  
      DF_DA_HORA_INI   (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR  8 DIGITS;        0000478602500000  
   IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000478702501000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          AND                   0000478802502000  
      DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000478902503000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                  0000479002504000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000479102505000  
     I_FECH_TER:= I_PROC_FECHA;                                         0000479202506000  
     I_HORA_TER:= I_PROC_HORA;                                          0000479302507000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_FEC_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR  8 DIGITS;           0000479402508000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_HRA_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR  8 DIGITS;           0000479502509000  
   END                                                                  0000479602510000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000479702511000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000479802512000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_FEC_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY                            0000479902513000  
      DF_DA_FECH_TER    (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR  8 DIGITS;        0000480002514000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_HRA_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY                            0000480102515000  
      I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                                   0000480202516000  
      DF_DA_HORA_TER   (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR  8 DIGITS;        0000480302517000  
   END;                                                                 0000480402518000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_ET_ANT_S    (I_IND_S) BY                              0000480502519000  
      I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                                   0000480602520000  
      DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS;        0000480702521000  
   IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000480802522000  
      DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          AND                   0000480902523000  
      DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000481002524000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                  0000481102525000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000481202526000  
      D_ET_ACTUAL:=                                                     0000481302527000  
               D_PROC_ET(DF_DA_FECH_INI(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),       0000481402528000  
                         DF_DA_HORA_INI(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),       0000481502529000  
                         I_FECH_TER                             ,       0000481602530000  
                         I_HORA_TER                             );      0000481702531000  
      REPLACE DF_AXP_ET_ACT_S    (I_IND_S) BY                           0000481802532000  
         I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=D_ET_ACTUAL) FOR 10 DIGITS;     0000481902533000  
   END                                                                  0000482002534000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000482102535000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000482202536000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_ET_ACT_S    (I_IND_S) BY                            0000482302537000  
         I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:= D_ET_ACTUAL:=                  0000482402538000  
         DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS;     0000482502539000  
   END;                                                                 0000482602540000  
   IF DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000482702541000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                  0000482802542000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000482902543000  
    IF DF_DP_PGM_TERMINADO(I_INDICE_PGM) = DF_DP_PT_ACTIVO THEN         0000483002544000  
     IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                      0000483102545000  
        DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                0000483202546000  
        IF I_PJEACT > 0 THEN                                            0000483302547000  
           REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY                      0000483402548000  
                   I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                      0000483502549000  
                   (D_ET_ACTUAL * ( 100 - I_PJEACT) / I_PJEACT))        0000483602550000  
                   FOR 10 DIGITS                                        0000483702551000  
        ELSE                                                            0000483802552000  
           REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY                      0000483902553000  
               I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                          0000484002554000  
               DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS0000484102555000  
     ELSE                                                               0000484202556000  
       IF I_PJEACT = 0 THEN                                             0000484302556100  
          REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY                       0000484402557000  
              I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:=                           0000484502557100  
              DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS 0000484602557200  
       ELSE                                                             0000484702557300  
          REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS       0000484802557400  
    ELSE                                                                0000484902558000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;           0000485002559000  
   END;                                                                 0000485102560000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_TXT_ADIC_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000485202561000  
      DF_DA_TXT_ADIC   (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)  FOR 40,               0000485302562000  
      " " FOR 2;                                                        0000485402563000  
   IF DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                      0000485502564000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_LIB                            THEN                 0000485602565000  
      IF DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) > 0 THEN             0000485702566000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000485802567000  
         REPLACE DF_AXP_TRANSXSEG_S(I_IND_S) BY                         0000485902568000  
                (DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) DIV        0000486002569000  
                 DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL_SE(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI))           0000486102570000  
                 FOR 8 DIGITS                                           0000486202571000  
              ,( INTEGERT (                                             0000486302572000  
                 DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) /          0000486402573000  
                 DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL_SE(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) * 100      0000486502574000

                 FOR 2 DIGITS;                                          0000486702576000  
         IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                             0000486802577000  
            DISPLAY ("L035: ACTXPROG: LLAMADOS: " !!                    0000486902578000

            " ET SEG: " !!                                              0000487102580000  
            STRING(DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL_SE(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),8));     0000487202581000  
      END                                                               0000487302582000  
      ELSE                                                              0000487402583000  
%        IF DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) > 0 THEN       0000487502584000

      REPLACE DF_AXP_TRANSXSEG_S(I_IND_S) BY "0" FOR 8;                 0000488002589000
