      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_LIB                            THEN                 0000488202591000  
      IF DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) > 0 THEN          0000488302592000

                (DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL_SE(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) DIV        0000488502594000

                 DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)            0000489002599000  
                 * 1000000                                              0000489102600000  
                 ))                                                     0000489202601000

      REPLACE DF_AXP_TPRMRESP_S(I_IND_S) BY "0" FOR 12;                 0000490002609000  
                                                                        0000490102610000

                DF_DA_EN_NUM_ACT (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 25      0000490502614000

      REPLACE DF_AXP_SEENCUENTRA (I_IND_S) BY " " FOR 25;               0000490702616000

   BEGIN                                                                0000491002616010  
    IF DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                       0000491102616015

    BEGIN                                                               0000491302616022  
      I_FECH_TER:= I_PROC_FECHA;                                        0000491402616024

      D_ET_ACTUAL:=                                                     0000491602616030

                        DF_DA_HORA_INI(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),        0000491802616040

                        I_HORA_TER                             )        0000492002616049  
    END                                                                 0000492102616050  
    ELSE                                                                0000492202616051  
      D_ET_ACTUAL:=                                                     0000492302616052

                        DF_DA_HORA_INI(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),        0000492502616054  
                        DF_DA_FECH_TER(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),        0000492602616055  
                        DF_DA_HORA_TER(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI));       0000492702616056  
    D_ET_ACTUAL:= D_ET_ACTUAL / 1000000;        % En segundos           0000492802616057  
    IF D_ET_ACTUAL > 0 THEN                                             0000492902616058  
    BEGIN                                                               0000493002616060  
       REPLACE DF_AXP_TXSXTRAN_S(I_IND_S) BY                            0000493102616065  
              (1 DIV D_ET_ACTUAL)                                       0000493202616070  
               FOR 8 DIGITS                                             0000493302616075  
            ,( INTEGERT (                                               0000493402616080  
               1 /                                                      0000493502616085  
               (D_ET_ACTUAL) * 100 ))                                   0000493602616090  
               FOR 2 DIGITS;                                            0000493702616100  
       REPLACE DF_AXP_TPRXTRAN_S(I_IND_S) BY                            0000493802616105  
                 (D_ET_ACTUAL DIV 1 )                                   0000493902616110  
                  FOR 6 DIGITS                                          0000494002616115  
               ,( INTEGERT (                                            0000494102616120  
                  (D_ET_ACTUAL) * 1000000                               0000494202616125  
                  ))                                                    0000494302616130  
                  FOR 6 DIGITS;                                         0000494402616135  
    END                                                                 0000494502616150  
    ELSE                                                                0000494602616250  
    BEGIN                                                               0000494702616350  
      REPLACE DF_AXP_TXSXTRAN_S(I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;            0000494802616450  
      REPLACE DF_AXP_TPRXTRAN_S(I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 12 DIGITS;            0000494902616550  
    END;                                                                0000495002616650  
   END                                                                  0000495102616700  
   ELSE                                                                 0000495202616750  
   BEGIN                                                                0000495302616800  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_TXSXTRAN_S(I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;             0000495402616850

 PROCEDURE PROGTERM;                                                    0000495902619000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000496002620000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_NUM_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY I_NUM_ACTI FOR 2 DIGITS;     0000496102621000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_STA_ACTI_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000496202622000

      DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV   (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) FOR  2 DIGITS;        0000496902629000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_FEC_INI_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;              0000497002630000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_HRA_INI_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;              0000497102631000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_FEC_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;              0000497202632000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_HRA_FIN_S   (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;              0000497302633000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_ET_ANT_S    (I_IND_S) BY                              0000497402634000  
      I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:= D_ET_ACTUAL:=                     0000497502635000  
      DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS;        0000497602636000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_ET_ACT_S    (I_IND_S) BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;             0000497702637000  
   IF DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                        0000497802638000  
      DF_DA_TPA_ACT_PROC                          THEN                  0000497902639000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000498002640000  
     REPLACE DF_AXP_TE_TERM_S   (I_IND_S) BY                            0000498102641000  
        I_PROC_MMSTOHHMSCS(D_TEM_HORA:= D_ET_ACTUAL:=                   0000498202642000  
        DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL  (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI)) FOR 10 DIGITS;      0000498302643000  
   END;                                                                 0000498402644000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_TXT_ADIC_S  (I_IND_S) BY                              0000498502645000  
      " " FOR 42;                                                       0000498602646000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_TRANSXSEG_S(I_IND_S) BY "0" FOR 8;                    0000498702647000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_TPRMRESP_S(I_IND_S) BY "0" FOR 12;                    0000498802648000  
                                                                        0000498902649000  
   REPLACE DF_AXP_SEENCUENTRA (I_IND_S) BY " " FOR 25;                  0000499002650000  
 END;                                                                   0000499102651000  
                                                                        0000499202652000  
 PROCEDURE ACTIXPROG;                                                   0000499302653000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000499402654000  
                                                                        0000499502655000  
  IF I_CUANTOS = 0 THEN                                                 0000499602656000  
     I_CUANTOS:= (SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 231;                         0000499702657000  
                                                                        0000499802658000  
  IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                                    0000499902659000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000500002660000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: USER CORRIDA: " !!                                  0000500102661000  
             STRING(I_USER_CORRIDA,*));                                 0000500202662000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: NUM PGM: " !!                                       0000500302663000  
             STRING(I_NUM_PGM,*));                                      0000500402664000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: COPIA: " !!                                         0000500502665000  
             STRING(I_NUM_COPIA,*));                                    0000500602666000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: TIPO PGM: " !!                                      0000500702667000  
             STRING(I_TIPO_PGM,*));                                     0000500802668000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: SE INICIA DESDE ACT > A: " !!                       0000500902669000  
             STRING(I_NUM_ACTI,*));                                     0000501002670000  
    DISPLAY ("L035: NO DE OCURRENCIAS: " !!                             0000501102671000  
             STRING(I_CUANTOS,*));                                      0000501202672000  
  END;                                                                  0000501302673000  
                                                                        0000501402674000  
  I_INDICE_PGM:= LA_PROGS[I_USER_CORRIDA,                               0000501502675000  
                          I_NUM_PGM,                                    0000501602676000  
                          I_NUM_COPIA,                                  0000501702677000  
                          I_TIPO_PGM];                                  0000501802678000  
                                                                        0000501902679000  
                                                                        0000502002680000  
  B_SIGUE:= TRUE;                                                       0000502102681000  
  WHILE I_IND_S <   I_CUANTOS AND                                       0000502202682000  
        B_SIGUE                   DO                                    0000502302683000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000502402684000  
    IF I_NUM_ACTI:= * + 1 LEQ 99 THEN                                   0000502502685000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000502602686000  
       IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                               0000502702687000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000502802688000  
         DISPLAY ("L035: ACTXPROG: ACTIVIDAD: " !!                      0000502902689000  
                  STRING(I_NUM_ACTI,4));                                0000503002690000  
         DISPLAY ("L035: ACTXPROG: STA ACTI: " !!                       0000503102691000  
               STRING(DF_DA_STA_ACTIV    (I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI),6)); 0000503202692000  
       END;                                                             0000503302693000  
       IF (DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =      % Valida     0000503402694000  
            DF_DA_STA_ACT_PROC                          OR  % actÀs que 0000503502695000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =      % existan    0000503602696000  
            DF_DA_STA_ACT_TERM                          OR              0000503702697000  
           DF_DA_STA_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =                   0000503802698000  
            DF_DA_STA_ACT_NOINI                           ) THEN        0000503902699000  
%          DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV(I_INDICE_PGM,I_NUM_ACTI) =      % Activ del  0000504002700000  
%                                                           % tpo sol   0000504102701000  
%           I_TIPO_ACTI                                     THEN        0000504202702000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000504302703000  
        IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                              0000504402704000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000504502705000  
          DISPLAY ("L035: ACTXPROG: ENCONTRE ACTI: " !!                 0000504602706000  
                   STRING(I_NUM_ACTI,4))                                0000504702707000  
        END;                                                            0000504802708000  
        IF DF_DP_NUM_CORRIDA (I_INDICE_PGM)                      =      0000504902709000  
           DF_AXU_NUM_CORRIDA(I_USER_CORRIDA,                           0000505002710000  
                              DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_INDICE_PGM)) OR     0000505102711000  
           DF_AXU_NUM_CORRIDA(I_USER_CORRIDA,                           0000505202712000  
                              DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_INDICE_PGM)) =      0000505302713000  
           0                                                     OR     0000505402714000  
           DF_DP_TIPO_DSPINFO (I_INDICE_PGM) =                          0000505502715000  
                              DF_DP_TPO_DSPINF_LIB               THEN   0000505602716000  
           PROGENPROC                                                   0000505702717000  
        ELSE                                                            0000505802718000  
           PROGTERM;                                                    0000505902719000  
                                                                        0000506002720000  
        I_IND_S:= * + 1;                                                0000506102721000  
       END;                                                             0000506202722000  
    END                                                                 0000506302723000  
    ELSE                                                                0000506402724000  
       B_SIGUE:= FALSE;                                                 0000506502725000  
  END;                                                                  0000506602726000  
                                                                        0000506702727000  
  REPLACE DF_AXP_NUM_ACTIARR_S BY I_IND_S FOR 2 DIGITS;                 0000506802728000  
                                                                        0000506902729000  
 END;                                                                   0000507002730000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000507102731000  
% OUTER BLOCK S038L035_33_ACTIXPROG_C                                   0000507202732000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000507302733000  
   REPLACE EA_SALIDA BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(EA_SALIDA);                     0000507402734000  
                                                                        0000507502735000  
%  B_DISPLAYS:= FALSE;                                                  0000507602736000  
                                                                        0000507702737000  
   IF SIZE(EA_ENTRADA) < 18 THEN                                        0000507802738000  
      I_RESULT:= 17;                                                    0000507902739000  
                                                                        0000508002740000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000508102741000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000508202742000  
     I_USER_CORRIDA:= IDF_AXP_USER_E;                                   0000508302743000  
     I_NUM_PGM     := IDF_AXP_PROG_E;                                   0000508402744000  
     I_NUM_COPIA   := IDF_AXP_NUM_COPIA_E;                              0000508502745000  
     I_TIPO_PGM    := IDF_AXP_TIPO_PROC_E;                              0000508602746000  
     I_CUANTOS     := IDF_AXP_CUANTOS_E;                                0000508702747000  
%    I_TIPO_ACTI   := IDF_AXP_TIPO_ACTI_E;                              0000508802748000  
     I_NUM_ACTI    := IDF_AXP_NUM_ACTI_E;                               0000508902749000  
                                                                        0000509002750000  
     IF I_CUANTOS > ((SIZE(EA_SALIDA) - 2) DIV 231) THEN                0000509102751000  
        I_RESULT:= 9;                                                   0000509202752000  
   END;                                                                 0000509302753000  
                                                                        0000509402754000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000509502755000  
      IF I_USER_CORRIDA < 1   OR                                        0000509602756000  
         I_USER_CORRIDA > 1000 THEN                                     0000509702757000  
         I_RESULT:= 10;                                                 0000509802758000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000509902759000  
      IF I_NUM_PGM <    1 OR                                            0000510002760000  
         I_NUM_PGM > 1000 THEN                                          0000510102761000  
         I_RESULT:= 11;                                                 0000510202762000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000510302763000  
      IF I_NUM_COPIA <   1 OR                                           0000510402764000  
         I_NUM_COPIA > 100 THEN                                         0000510502765000  
         I_RESULT:= 1;                                                  0000510602766000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000510702767000  
      IF I_TIPO_PGM < 1 OR                                              0000510802768000  
         I_TIPO_PGM > 2 THEN                                            0000510902769000  
         I_RESULT:= 2;                                                  0000511002770000  
%  IF NOT (I_TIPO_ACTI = 1 OR                                           0000511102771000  
%          I_TIPO_ACTI = 2   ) THEN                                     0000511202772000  
%     I_RESULT:= 6;                                                     0000511302773000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000511402774000  
      IF I_NUM_ACTI > 99 THEN                                           0000511502775000  
         I_RESULT:= 4;                                                  0000511602776000  
                                                                        0000511702777000  
   IF I_RESULT = 0 THEN                                                 0000511802778000  
      ACTIXPROG;                                                        0000511902779000  
                                                                        0000512002780000  
   S038L035_33_ACTIXPROG_C:= I_RESULT;                                  0000512102781000  
                                                                        0000512202782000  
%  B_DISPLAYS:= FALSE;                                                  0000512302783000  
                                                                        0000512402784000  
 END S038L035_33_ACTIXPROG_C;                                           0000512502785000

%********************************************************************** 0000514802808000  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE PROC_INICIO;                                         0000514902809000  
%********************************************************************** 0000515002810000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000515102811000  
   REAL R_DMERROR;                                                      0000515202812000  
   BOOLEAN B_ERROR;                                                     0000515302813000  
                                                                        0000515402814000  
 PROCEDURE CARGABASE;                                                   0000515502815000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000515602816000  
   BOOLEAN B_HAY_PROGS,                                                 0000515702817000  
           B_HAY_ACTS;                                                  0000515802818000  
   INTEGER I_IND_PGM,                                                   0000515902819000  
           I_IND_ACT;                                                   0000516002820000  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EA_NOM_ACTIV,                                           0000516102821000  
                EA_TXT_ADIC[0:39],                                      0000516202822000  
                EA_NOM_TPO_PROC[0:39],                                  0000516302823000  
                EA_NOM_PGM[0:79];                                       0000516402824000  
                                                                        0000516502825000  
 PROCEDURE CARGA_ACTIVIDADES;                                           0000516602826000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000516702827000  
                                                                        0000516802828000  
   B_HAY_ACTS:= TRUE;                                                   0000516902829000  
   FIND FIRST B03SXIDACT AT                                             0000517002830000  
        B03-USER-CORRIDA = DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM) AND           0000517102831000  
        B03-NUM-PGM      = DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM) AND           0000517202832000  
        B03-NUM-COPIA    = DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM) AND           0000517302833000  
        B03-TIPO-PGM     = DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM):R_DMERROR;    0000517402834000  
   IF NOT BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                       0000517502835000  
      WHILE B_HAY_ACTS DO                                               0000517602836000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000517702837000  
                                                                        0000517802838000  
        GET S038B03ACTIVIDAD (I_IND_ACT         := B03-NUM-ACTIVIDAD);  0000517902839000  
                                                                        0000518002840000  
        IF I_IND_ACT > 0     AND                                        0000518102841000  
           I_IND_ACT LEQ 100 THEN                                       0000518202842000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000518302843000  
          GET S038B03ACTIVIDAD                                          0000518402844000  
         (DF_DA_USER_CORRIDA(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-USER-CORRIDA    0000518502845000  
         ,DF_DA_NUM_PGM     (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-NUM-PGM         0000518602846000  
         ,DF_DA_NUM_COPIA   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-NUM-COPIA       0000518702847000  
         ,DF_DA_TIPO_PGM    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-TIPO-PGM        0000518802848000  
         ,DF_DA_NUM_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-NUM-ACTIVIDAD   0000518902849000  
         ,EA_NOM_ACTIV                           := B03-NOM-ACTIVIDAD   0000519002850000  
         ,DF_DA_STA_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-ESTATUS-ACT     0000519102851000  
         ,DF_DA_REGS_APROC  (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-REGS-APROC      0000519202852000  
         ,DF_DA_NUM_LLAMADOS(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-NUM-LLAMADOS    0000519302853000  
         ,DF_DA_TPO_ACTIV   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-TPO-ACTIVIDAD   0000519402854000  
         ,DF_DA_FECH_INI    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-FECH-INICIO     0000519502855000  
         ,DF_DA_HORA_INI    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-HORA-INICIO     0000519602856000  
         ,DF_DA_FECH_TER    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-FECH-TERMINA    0000519702857000  
         ,DF_DA_HORA_TER    (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-HORA-TERMINA    0000519802858000  
         ,DF_DA_ET_ANTERIOR (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-ET-ANTERIOR     0000519902859000  
         ,DF_DA_ET_ACTUAL   (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT):= B03-ET-ACTUAL       0000520002860000  
         ,EA_TXT_ADIC                            := B03-TXT-ADIC     ); 0000520102861000  
                                                                        0000520202862000  
         REPLACE DF_DA_NOM_ACTIV(I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) BY                0000520302863000  
                 EA_NOM_ACTIV FOR 40,                                   0000520402864000  
                 "  ";                                                  0000520502865000  
                                                                        0000520602866000  
         REPLACE DF_DA_TXT_ADIC (I_IND_PGM,I_IND_ACT) BY                0000520702867000  
                 EA_TXT_ADIC  FOR 40,                                   0000520802868000  
                 "  ";                                                  0000520902869000  
        END                                                             0000521002870000  
        ELSE                                                            0000521102871000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000521202872000  
%         DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                 0000521302873000  
%         REPLACE EA_MSG BY "ERROR,AL CARGAR BASE ENCONTRE INDICES DE ",0000521402874000  
%                           I_IND_ACT FOR 4 DIGITS;                     0000521502875000  
%         DF_DISPLAY(EA_MSG);                                           0000521602876000  
          REPLACE MENS1[0] BY                                           0000521702876100  
            "ERROR,AL CARGAR BASE ENCONTRE INDICES DE ",                0000521802876200  
            I_IND_ACT FOR 4 DIGITS;                                     0000521902876300  
          LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                               0000522002876400  
        END;                                                            0000522102877000  
                                                                        0000522202878000  
        FIND NEXT B03SXIDACT AT                                         0000522302879000  
            B03-USER-CORRIDA = DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000522402880000  
            B03-NUM-PGM      = DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000522502881000  
            B03-NUM-COPIA    = DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM) AND       0000522602882000  
            B03-TIPO-PGM     = DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM):R_DMERROR;0000522702883000  
        IF BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                      0000522802884000  
           B_HAY_ACTS:= FALSE;                                          0000522902885000  
      END;                                                              0000523002886000  
 END;                                                                   0000523102887000  
                                                                        0000523202888000  
 PROCEDURE CARGA_PROGRAMAS;                                             0000523302889000  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000523402890000  
   B_HAY_PROGS:= TRUE;                                                  0000523502891000  
   FIND FIRST S038B02PROGRAMAS:R_DMERROR;                               0000523602892000  
   IF NOT BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                       0000523702893000  
      WHILE B_HAY_PROGS DO                                              0000523802894000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000523902895000  
                                                                        0000524002896000  
        GET S038B02PROGRAMAS(I_IND_PGM : = B02-INDICE-PGM);             0000524102896100  
        IF I_IND_PGM GEQ SIZE(LA_DATOS_PROGS[*,*]) THEN                 0000524202896300  
        BEGIN                                                           0000524302896400  
          RESIZE(LA_DATOS_PROGS  [*,*],(I_IND_PGM + 1), RETAIN);        0000524402898000  
          RESIZE(LA_DATOS_ACT  [*,*,*],(I_IND_PGM + 1), RETAIN);        0000524502899000  
        END;                                                            0000524602899100  
                                                                        0000524702900000  
        GET S038B02PROGRAMAS                                            0000524802901000  
            (DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM):= B02-USER-CORRIDA          0000524902902000  
            ,DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM):= B02-NUM-PGM               0000525002903000  
            ,DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM):= B02-NUM-COPIA             0000525102904000  
            ,DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM):= B02-TIPO-PGM              0000525202905000  
            ,EA_NOM_PGM                    := B02-NOM-PGM               0000525302906000  
            ,DF_DP_PGM_TERMINADO(I_IND_PGM):= B02-ESTATUS-PGM           0000525402907000  
            ,DF_DP_TIPO_DSPINFO (I_IND_PGM):= B02-TIPO-DSPINFO          0000525502908000  
            ,DF_DP_FECH_INI     (I_IND_PGM):= B02-FECH-INICIO           0000525602909000  
            ,DF_DP_HORA_INI     (I_IND_PGM):= B02-HORA-INICIO           0000525702910000  
            ,DF_DP_FECH_TER     (I_IND_PGM):= B02-FECH-TERMINA          0000525802911000  
            ,DF_DP_HORA_TER     (I_IND_PGM):= B02-HORA-TERMINA          0000525902912000  
            ,DF_DP_NUM_MIX      (I_IND_PGM):= B02-NUM-MIX               0000526002913000  
            ,DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM):= B02-NUM-TPO-PROC          0000526102914000  
            ,EA_NOM_TPO_PROC               := B02-NOM-TPO-PROC          0000526202915000  
            ,DF_DP_ET_ANTERIOR  (I_IND_PGM):= B02-ET-ANTERIOR           0000526302916000  
            ,DF_DP_ET_ACTUAL    (I_IND_PGM):= B02-ET-ACTUAL             0000526402917000  
            ,DF_DP_NUM_CORRIDA  (I_IND_PGM):= B02-NUM-CORRIDA           0000526502918000  
            ,DF_DP_INIGPO       (I_IND_PGM):= B02-INICIO-GPO  );        0000526602919000  
                                                                        0000526702920000  
        REPLACE DF_DP_NOM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM) BY                       0000526802921000  
                EA_NOM_PGM FOR 80,                                      0000526902922000  
                "    ";                                                 0000527002923000  
        REPLACE DF_DP_NOM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM) BY                       0000527102924000  
                EA_NOM_TPO_PROC FOR 40,                                 0000527202925000  
                "  ";                                                   0000527302926000  
                                                                        0000527402927000  
        LA_PROGS[DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),                        0000527502928000  
                 DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM),                        0000527602929000  
                 DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM),                        0000527702930000  
                 DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM)]:= I_IND_PGM;           0000527802931000  
                                                                        0000527902931050  
        IF B_DISPLAYS THEN                                              0000528002931100  
        BEGIN                                                           0000528102931150  
          DISPLAY ("L035: USER: "!!                                     0000528202931200  
                   STRING(DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),*));           0000528302931250  
          DISPLAY ("L035: PGM: "!!                                      0000528402931300  
                   STRING(DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM),*));           0000528502931350  
          DISPLAY ("L035: COPIA: "!!                                    0000528602931400  
                   STRING(DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM),*));           0000528702931450  
          DISPLAY ("L035: TPO PGM: "!!                                  0000528802931500  
                   STRING(DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM),*));           0000528902931550  
          DISPLAY ("L035: IND PGM: "!!                                  0000529002931600  
                   STRING(I_IND_PGM,*));                                0000529102931650  
        END;                                                            0000529202931700  
                                                                        0000529302932000  
        LA_USERS[DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM)]:= 1;                   0000529402933000  
                                                                        0000529502934000  
        LA_PROGXAGRUP[DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),                   0000529602935000  
                      DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM),                   0000529702936000  
                      DF_DP_NUM_PGM      (I_IND_PGM),                   0000529802937000  
                      DF_DP_NUM_COPIA    (I_IND_PGM),                   0000529902938000  
                      DF_DP_TIPO_PGM     (I_IND_PGM)] := I_IND_PGM;     0000530002939000  
                                                                        0000530102940000  
        % Se llenan datos de arreglo de Agrupadores X User              0000530202941000  
        % Si es inicio de gpo se inicializan datos                      0000530302942000  
         DF_AXU_NUM_TPO_PROC(DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),            0000530402943000  
                             DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)):=          0000530502944000  
                             DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM);            0000530602945000  
                                                                        0000530702946000  
        % Solo si es inicio de gpo se reemplaza el nombre del grupo     0000530802947000  
        IF DF_DP_INIGPO (I_IND_PGM) > 0 THEN                            0000530902948000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000531002949000  
          REPLACE DF_AXU_NOM_TPO_PROC(DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),   0000531102950000  
                                      DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)) BY0000531202951000  
                  DF_DP_NOM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM) FOR 42;                0000531302952000  
          DF_AXU_NUM_CORRIDA(DF_DP_USER_CORRIDA (I_IND_PGM),            0000531402953000  
                             DF_DP_NUM_TPO_PROC (I_IND_PGM)):=          0000531502954000  
                  DF_DP_NUM_CORRIDA  (I_IND_PGM);                       0000531602955000  
        END;                                                            0000531702956000  
                                                                        0000531802957000  
        CARGA_ACTIVIDADES;                                              0000531902958000  
                                                                        0000532002959000  
        FIND NEXT S038B02PROGRAMAS:R_DMERROR;                           0000532102960000  
        IF BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                      0000532202961000  
           B_HAY_PROGS:= FALSE;                                         0000532302962000  
      END;                                                              0000532402963000  
 END;                                                                   0000532502964000  
                                                                        0000532602965000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000532702966000  
% OUTER BLOCK CARGABASE                                                 0000532802967000  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000532902968000  
 CARGA_PROGRAMAS;                                                       0000533002969000  
                                                                        0000533102970000  
 END;                                                                   0000533202971000

%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000533402973000  
% OUTER BLOCK PROC_INICIO                                               0000533502974000

   OPEN UPDATE S500BD05MAPLI:R_DMERROR;                                 00005337          
   IF BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                           0000533802977000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000533902978000  
%    DF_LIMPIAMSG;                                                      0000534002979000  
%    REPLACE EA_MSG BY "ERROR AL ABRIR LA BASE DE DATOS";               0000534102980000

     REPLACE MENS1[0] BY "ERROR AL ABRIR LA BASE DE DATOS";             0000534302981100  
     LJ(CONT,MENS1);                                                    0000534402981200  
     B_ERROR:= TRUE;                                                    0000534502982000  
   END;                                                                 0000534602983000  
   IF NOT B_ERROR THEN                                                  0000534702984000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000534802985000  
     FIND FIRST S038B01CONTROL:R_DMERROR;                               0000534902986000  
     IF NOT BOOLEAN(R_DMERROR) THEN                                     0000535002987000  
        GET S038B01CONTROL ( I_B01_NUM_NODO  := B01-NUM-NODO            0000535102988000  
                           ,EA_B01_NOM_EQUIPO:= B01-NOM-EQUIPO)         0000535202989000  
   END;                                                                 0000535302990000  
   IF NOT B_ERROR THEN                                                  0000535402991000  
     CARGABASE;                                                         0000535502992000  
 END;                                                                   0000535602993000
